// =======================================================

"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Camera, CheckCircle2, Upload, Activity, Scale, Shield, User, Briefcase, Copy, Key } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { dashboardAPI } from '@/services/api';

export default function ProvisioningForm() {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [activeDay, setActiveDay] = useState('Day 1');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Generated Credentials State (ID + Random Temp Password)
  const [generatedCreds, setGeneratedCreds] = useState<{
    userCode: string;
    tempPassword: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  // Role Selector
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'CLIENT' | 'COACH'>('CLIENT');

  // Coach Fields
  const [coachName, setCoachName] = useState('');
  const [coachEmail, setCoachEmail] = useState('');
  const [coachSpecialization, setCoachSpecialization] = useState('hypertrophy');

  // Client Fields
  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState('');
  const [clientEmail, setClientEmail] = useState('');
  const [age, setAge] = useState('');
  const [height, setHeight] = useState('');
  const [weight, setWeight] = useState('');
  const [activityLevel, setActivityLevel] = useState('sedentary');
  const [primaryGoal, setPrimaryGoal] = useState('fat_loss');
  const [medicalNotes, setMedicalNotes] = useState('');

  // Shared Styles (Preserved Intact)
  const inputStyle = {
    width: '100%',
    padding: '14px',
    background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)',
    border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
    outline: 'none',
    fontSize: '15px',
    marginTop: '8px',
    transition: 'border-color 0.2s ease'
  };

  const selectBg = 'url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=US-ASCII,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%22292.4%22%20height%3D%22292.4%22%3E%3Cpath%20fill%3D%22%23888888%22%20d%3D%22M287%2069.4a17.6%2017.6%200%200%200-13-5.4H18.4c-5%200-9.3%201.8-12.9%205.4A17.6%2017.6%200%200%200%200%2082.2c0%205%201.8%209.3%205.4%2012.9l128%20127.9c3.6%203.6%207.8%205.4%2012.8%205.4s9.2-1.8%2012.8-5.4L287%2095c3.5-3.5%205.4-7.8%205.4-12.8%200-5-1.9-9.2-5.5-12.8z%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E")';

  const days = ['Day 1', 'Day 2', 'Day 3'];
  const meals = ['Breakfast', 'Brunch', 'Lunch', 'Snacks', 'Dinner'];

  const provisionAccount = async (quickProvisionOnly = false) => {
    setError(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const payload = role === 'COACH'
      ? {
          role: 'COACH',
          name: coachName,
          email: coachEmail,
          specialization: coachSpecialization,
        }
      : {
          role: 'CLIENT',
          name: clientName,
          email: clientEmail,
          ...(age ? { age: Number(age) } : {}),
          ...(height ? { height: Number(height) } : {}),
          ...(weight ? { weight: Number(weight) } : {}),
          activityLevel,
          primaryGoal,
          medicalNotes,
          onboardingCompleted: !quickProvisionOnly && Boolean(age && height && weight),
        };

    try {
      // The server generates the ID and a one-time temporary password (only its hash is stored)
      const data = await dashboardAPI.saveOnboarding(payload);
      setGeneratedCreds({ userCode: data.userCode, tempPassword: data.tempPassword });
      setSubmitted(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Never invent credentials: if the server did not create the account, say so
      setError(err.message || 'Could not provision this account. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    await provisionAccount(false);
  };

  const handleCopyCredentials = () => {
    if (!generatedCreds) return;
    const targetName = role === 'COACH' ? coachName : clientName;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(
      `The Blue Fit Login Credentials\nName: ${targetName}\nRole: ${role}\nUser ID: ${generatedCreds.userCode}\nTemporary Password: ${generatedCreds.tempPassword}`
    );
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '600px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', minHeight: '80vh' }}>
        <div className="app-card fade-in" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px 20px', background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)' }}>
          <div style={{ width: '80px', height: '80px', background: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.1)', border: '1px solid var(--accent-green)', borderRadius: '50%', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', margin: '0 auto 24px auto', boxShadow: '0 0 15px rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.2)' }}>
            <CheckCircle2 className="neon-green" size={40} color="#00E27B" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="glazy-title" style={{ fontSize: '28px', marginBottom: '12px', textTransform: 'none' }}>
            {role === 'COACH' ? 'Coach Provisioned' : 'Client Provisioned'}
          </h2>
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: '24px', fontSize: '15px' }}>
            Share these credentials with <strong style={{ color: '#fff' }}>{role === 'COACH' ? coachName : clientName}</strong>. They will be prompted to change this temporary password and complete onboarding on first sign-in.
          </p>

          {/* Generated User ID & Temporary Password Cards */}
          {generatedCreds && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px', marginBottom: '24px', textAlign: 'left' }}>
              <div className="plan-card" style={{ padding: '16px 20px' }}>
                <div className="plan-card-left">
                  <span className="plan-item-category">Assigned User ID</span>
                  <span className="plan-item-name" style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--accent-gold)', letterSpacing: '1px' }}>
                    {generatedCreds.userCode}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="plan-item-category">{role}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="plan-card" style={{ padding: '16px 20px' }}>
                <div className="plan-card-left">
                  <span className="plan-item-category">One-Time Temporary Password</span>
                  <span className="plan-item-name" style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--accent-green)', letterSpacing: '1px' }}>
                    {generatedCreds.tempPassword}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Key size={20} color="var(--accent-green)" />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopyCredentials}
              className="btn-secondary"
              style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
            >
              <Copy size={16} /> {copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy ID & Password'}
            </button>
            <Link href="/admin" className="btn-primary" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', textDecoration: 'none', textAlign: 'center' }}>
              Return to Admin Portal
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
      <div className="app-card fade-in">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="section-header" style={{ flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '4px', marginBottom: '32px' }}>
          <h1 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '32px', textTransform: 'none' }}>System Provisioning</h1>
          <p className="label-text" style={{ textTransform: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', letterSpacing: '0', fontSize: '14px' }}>Create and configure platform access credentials.</p>
        </div>

        {/* Stepper only visible for Clients */}
        {role === 'CLIENT' && (
          <div className="progress-container">
            <div className="progress-line">
              <div className="progress-line-fill" style={{ width: currentStep === 1 ? '50%' : '100%' }}></div>
            </div>
            <div className="progress-step">
              <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active' : ''}`}></div>
              <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Metrics</span>
            </div>
            <div className="progress-step">
              <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active' : ''}`}></div>
              <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>3-Day Log</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
          {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}
          
          {/* STEP 1: Main Data Entry */}
          {currentStep === 1 && (
            <div className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
              
              {/* Role Selection Dropdown */}
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                  <Shield size={18} color="#60A5FA" /> Account Provisioning
                </h3>
                <div>
                  <label className="label-text">Select Account Type</label>
                  <select 
                    value={role}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      setRole(e.target.value as 'CLIENT' | 'COACH');
                      setCurrentStep(1);
                    }}
                    required 
                    style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                  >
                    <option value="CLIENT" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Athlete / Client</option>
                    <option value="COACH" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Coach / Trainer</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* COACH FORM BRANCH */}
              {role === 'COACH' && (
                <div className="fade-in" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                  <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                    <Briefcase size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Coach Profile
                  </h3>
                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                    <div>
                      <label className="label-text">Full Name</label>
                      <input 
                        type="text" 
                        required 
                        value={coachName}
                        onChange={(e) => setCoachName(e.target.value)}
                        style={inputStyle} 
                        placeholder="e.g. Marcus Vance" 
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="label-text">Email Address</label>
                      <input 
                        type="email" 
                        required 
                        value={coachEmail}
                        onChange={(e) => setCoachEmail(e.target.value)}
                        style={inputStyle} 
                        placeholder="coach@thebluefit.com" 
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}>
                      <label className="label-text">Primary Specialization</label>
                      <select 
                        value={coachSpecialization}
                        onChange={(e) => setCoachSpecialization(e.target.value)}
                        style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                      >
                        <option value="hypertrophy" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Muscle Hypertrophy & Bodybuilding</option>
                        <option value="nutrition" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Clinical Nutrition & Fat Loss</option>
                        <option value="mobility" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Mobility & Injury Rehab</option>
                        <option value="powerlifting" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Strength & Powerlifting</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: '24px', width: '100%' }}>
                    {isSubmitting ? 'Provisioning...' : 'Provision Coach Credentials'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* CLIENT FORM BRANCH */}
              {role === 'CLIENT' && (
                <div className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                    <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                      <User size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Basic Identity
                    </h3>
                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Full Name</label>
                        <input 
                          type="text" 
                          required 
                          value={clientName}
                          onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
                          style={inputStyle} 
                          placeholder="e.g. Suresh" 
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Email Address</label>
                        <input 
                          type="email" 
                          required 
                          value={clientEmail}
                          onChange={(e) => setClientEmail(e.target.value)}
                          style={inputStyle} 
                          placeholder="client@email.com" 
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Quick Provision Button: Generates ID & Temp Password immediately so Client fills metrics on 1st login */}
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isSubmitting || !clientName.trim() || !clientEmail.trim()}
                      onClick={() => provisionAccount(true)}
                      className="btn-secondary"
                      style={{ marginTop: '20px', width: '100%', border: '1px solid rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.4)', color: 'var(--accent-gold)' }}
                    >
                      {isSubmitting ? 'Generating...' : 'Quick Provision ID & Temp Password (Client Fills Metrics on 1st Login)'}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                    <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                      <Scale size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Physical Attributes
                    </h3>
                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Age</label>
                        <input 
                          type="number" 
                          required 
                          value={age}
                          onChange={(e) => setAge(e.target.value)}
                          style={inputStyle} 
                          placeholder="e.g. 28" 
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Height (cm)</label>
                        <input 
                          type="number" 
                          step="0.1" 
                          required 
                          value={height}
                          onChange={(e) => setHeight(e.target.value)}
                          style={inputStyle} 
                          placeholder="e.g. 175" 
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Current Weight (kg)</label>
                        <input 
                          type="number" 
                          step="0.1" 
                          required 
                          value={weight}
                          onChange={(e) => setWeight(e.target.value)}
                          style={inputStyle} 
                          placeholder="e.g. 82.5" 
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                    <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                      <Activity size={18} color="#60A5FA" /> Lifestyle & Health
                    </h3>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Daily Activity Level</label>
                        <select 
                          value={activityLevel}
                          onChange={(e) => setActivityLevel(e.target.value)}
                          style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                        >
                          <option value="sedentary" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Sedentary (Desk job, minimal movement)</option>
                          <option value="moderate" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Moderately Active (3-5 workouts/week)</option>
                          <option value="high" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Very Active (Heavy training / active work)</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Primary Fitness Goal</label>
                        <select 
                          value={primaryGoal}
                          onChange={(e) => setPrimaryGoal(e.target.value)}
                          style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                        >
                          <option value="fat_loss" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Fat Loss & Definition</option>
                          <option value="hypertrophy" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Muscle Hypertrophy</option>
                          <option value="recomp" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Body Recomposition</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="label-text">Medical Notes / Limitations</label>
                        <textarea 
                          rows={3} 
                          value={medicalNotes}
                          onChange={(e) => setMedicalNotes(e.target.value)}
                          style={{ ...inputStyle, resize: 'none' }} 
                          placeholder="List any past injuries or dietary restrictions..."
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setCurrentStep(2)} className="btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>
                    Continue to Food Log
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 2: 3-Day Diet Recall (Clients Only) */}
          {currentStep === 2 && role === 'CLIENT' && (
            <div className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
              
              <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
                {days.map((day) => (
                  <button 
                    key={day}
                    type="button"
                    className={activeDay === day ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}
                    onClick={() => setActiveDay(day)}
                    style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1 }}
                  >
                    {day}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                  <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '600', margin: 0 }}>
                    <Camera size={18} color="#60A5FA" /> {activeDay} Dietary Recall
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                  {meals.map((meal) => (
                    <label 
                      key={`${activeDay}-${meal}`} 
                      style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '140px', border: '2px dashed var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)', cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'all 0.2s ease', textAlign: 'center', padding: '12px' }}
                    >
                      <Upload size={24} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ marginBottom: '12px' }} />
                      <span className="label-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginBottom: '4px' }}>{meal}</span>
                      <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setCurrentStep(1)} className="btn-secondary">
                  Back
                </button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary">
                  {isSubmitting ? 'Saving Profile...' : 'Save Assessment & Generate ID'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Shield, Users, Activity, Database, Plus, Edit2, Trash2, Key, PieChart, Utensils } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { dashboardAPI } from '@/services/api';

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'users' | 'services' | 'analytics'>('users');
  const [dbUsers, setDbUsers] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [fetchError, setFetchError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  // NEW: State for the right-side glassmorphism drawer

  // Fetch live users from DynamoDB on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchUsers = async () => {
      try {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setFetchError(null);
        
        const data = await dashboardAPI.getAllUsers();
        setDbUsers(data);
      } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("Fetch failed:", error);
        setFetchError(error.message || "Failed to load users.");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    
    fetchUsers();
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteUser = async (userId: string) => {
    if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to delete this user?")) return;
    
    try {
      await dashboardAPI.deleteUser(userId);

      setDbUsers((prevUsers) => prevUsers.filter((user) => user.PK !== userId));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error deleting user:", error);
      alert("Failed to delete user. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  const handleAssignCoach = async (clientId: string, selectedCoachId: string) => {
    if (!selectedCoachId) return;

    try {
      await dashboardAPI.assignCoach(clientId, selectedCoachId);

      setDbUsers((prevUsers) => 
        prevUsers.map((user) => 
          user.PK === clientId ? { ...user, coachId: selectedCoachId } : user
        )
      );
      alert("Coach assigned successfully!");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error assigning coach:", error);
      alert("Failed to assign coach.");
    }
  };

  const coaches = dbUsers.filter(u => u.role === 'COACH');

  const stats = [
    { label: 'Total Users', value: dbUsers.length.toString(), icon: Users, glowColor: 'var(--text-primary)' },
    { label: 'Active Coaches', value: coaches.length.toString(), icon: Shield, glowColor: '#60A5FA' },
    { label: 'Generated Diets', value: '840', icon: Utensils, glowColor: 'var(--accent-gold)' },
    { label: 'System Health', value: '99.9%', icon: Activity, glowColor: 'var(--accent-green)', isPulse: true },
  ];

  return (
    <>

      {/* Main Admin Dashboard Content */}
      <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1000px' }}>
        <div className="app-card fade-in">
          
          <div className="section-header" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: '32px' }}>
            <div>
              <h1 className="glazy-title" style={{ fontSize: '32px', textTransform: 'none', marginBottom: '8px', margin: 0 }}>Master Admin Portal</h1>
              <p className="label-text" style={{ textTransform: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', letterSpacing: '0', fontSize: '14px', margin: 0 }}>Global system oversight, user access management, and core services.</p>
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()} 
              className="btn-primary" 
              style={{ padding: '10px 20px', flex: 'none', fontSize: '13px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', background: 'rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.15)', color: 'var(--accent-gold)', border: '1px solid rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.4)', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            >
              <Database size={16} /> Sync Database
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="results-summary" style={{ marginBottom: '32px' }}>
            <div className="macro-row" style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))', gap: '12px' }}>
              {stats.map((stat, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="macro-pill" style={{ flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', padding: '16px', gap: '12px' }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', width: '100%' }}>
                    <stat.icon size={18} color={stat.glowColor} />
                    <span className="plan-item-category" style={{ margin: 0 }}>{stat.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  <strong className={stat.isPulse ? "pulse-text" : ""} style={{ fontSize: '24px', color: stat.glowColor }}>
                    {stat.value}
                  </strong>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="button-row admin-tabs" style={{ marginBottom: '24px', flex: 'none', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', paddingBottom: '16px' }}>
            <button 
              className={`${activeTab === 'users' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'} admin-tab`} 
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('users')}
              style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
            >
              <Users size={16} /> Directory (Access)
            </button>
            <button 
              className={`${activeTab === 'services' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'} admin-tab`} 
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('services')}
              style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
            >
              <Utensils size={16} /> Core Services
            </button>
            <button 
              className={`${activeTab === 'analytics' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'} admin-tab`} 
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('analytics')}
              style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
            >
              <PieChart size={16} /> Monthly Analysis
            </button>
          </div>

          {activeTab === 'users' && (
            <div className="fade-in">
              <div className="section-header" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '16px' }}>User Directory</h2>
                <Link href="/admin/onboarding" className="btn-secondary" style={{ padding: '8px 16px', fontSize: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
                  <Plus size={14} /> Provision User
                </Link>
              </div>
              
              <div className="plan-cards-container" style={{ maxHeight: '500px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                {isLoading ? (
                  <div style={{ padding: '24px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Syncing Database...</div>
                ) : fetchError ? (
                  <div style={{ padding: '24px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#FF453A', background: 'rgba(255, 69, 58, 0.1)', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid rgba(255, 69, 58, 0.3)' }}>
                    Error: {fetchError}. Make sure your API is deployed and the URL in your .env file is correct.
                  </div>
                ) : dbUsers.length === 0 ? (
                  <div style={{ padding: '24px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>No users found. Provision one to get started.</div>
                ) : (
                  dbUsers.map((user) => (
                    <div key={user.PK} className="plan-card user-row">
                      <div className="plan-card-left">
                        <span className="plan-item-name" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                          {user.name || 'Unnamed User'}
                          {user.role === 'COACH' && <Shield size={14} color="#60A5FA" />}
                        </span>
                        <span className="plan-item-category">
                          {user.role || 'UNKNOWN'} • ID: <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>{user.PK ? user.PK.split('#')[1] : 'N/A'}</span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="plan-card-right user-row-actions" style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        {user.role === 'CLIENT' && (
                          <select
                            value={user.coachId || ""}
                            onChange={(e) => handleAssignCoach(user.PK, e.target.value)}
                            style={{
                              background: 'var(--bg-input)',
                              border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
                              color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                              padding: '6px 10px',
                              borderRadius: '8px',
                              fontSize: '12px',
                              cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                          >
                            <option value="" disabled>Assign Coach...</option>
                            {coaches.map(coach => (
                              <option key={coach.PK} value={coach.PK}>{coach.name}</option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        )}

                        <button style={{ background: 'rgba(0, 122, 255, 0.1)', border: '1px solid rgba(0, 122, 255, 0.3)', color: '#60A5FA', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer' }} title="Manage Access Level">
                          <Key size={14} />
                        </button>
                        <button style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', color: 'var(--text-primary)', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer' }} title="Edit User">
                          <Edit2 size={14} />
                        </button>

                        <button 
                          onClick={() => handleDeleteUser(user.PK)}
                          style={{ background: 'rgba(255, 69, 58, 0.1)', border: '1px solid rgba(255, 69, 58, 0.3)', color: '#FF453A', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer' }} 
                          title="Delete/Suspend User"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'services' && (
            <div className="fade-in">
               <div className="section-header" style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '16px' }}>Master Tools</h2>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                
                <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                  <div style={{ width: '48px', height: '48px', background: 'rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.1)', borderRadius: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', border: '1px solid rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.3)' }}>
                    <Utensils className="neon-orange" size={24} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginBottom: '8px' }}>Master Diet Orchestrator</h3>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', lineHeight: '1.5' }}>Access the core AWS generation engine to build and assign universal diet plans bypassing client restrictions.</p>
                  </div>
                  <Link href="/client/diet" className="btn-secondary" style={{ textAlign: 'center', textDecoration: 'none', marginTop: 'auto' }}>
                    Launch Orchestrator
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'analytics' && (
            <div className="fade-in">
              <div className="section-header" style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '16px' }}>Platform Analytics</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="error-banner" style={{ background: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.3)', color: '#FFF', textAlign: 'left', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                <strong style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: '8px', color: 'var(--accent-green)' }}>Monthly Report Ready</strong>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                  System-wide analysis is complete. Client retention is up 12% and average adherence sits at 78%.
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
==> src/app/client/check-in/page.tsx <==
"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Camera, Scale, TrendingUp, CheckCircle2, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { dashboardAPI } from '@/services/api';

export default function WeeklyCheckIn() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    const form = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    const waist = String(form.get('waist') || '').trim();
    const notes = String(form.get('notes') || '').trim();
    try {
      await dashboardAPI.saveCheckin({
        weight: Number(form.get('weight')),
        adherence: String(form.get('adherence')),
        workoutQuality: String(form.get('workoutQuality')),
        ...(waist ? { waist: Number(waist) } : {}),
        ...(notes ? { notes } : {}),
      });
      setSubmitted(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Could not save your check-in. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // Shared dark-mode input styling
  const inputStyle = {
    width: '100%',
    padding: '14px',
    background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)',
    border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
    outline: 'none',
    fontSize: '15px',
    marginTop: '8px',
    transition: 'border-color 0.2s ease'
  };

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '600px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', minHeight: '80vh' }}>
        <div className="app-card fade-in" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px 20px', background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)' }}>
          <div style={{ width: '80px', height: '80px', background: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.1)', border: '1px solid var(--accent-green)', borderRadius: '50%', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', margin: '0 auto 24px auto', boxShadow: '0 0 15px rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.2)' }}>
            <CheckCircle2 className="neon-green" size={40} color="#00E27B" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '28px', marginBottom: '12px', textTransform: 'none' }}>Check-In Complete</h2>
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: '32px', fontSize: '15px' }}>Your coach has been notified and will review your progress shortly.</p>
          <button onClick={() => router.push('/client')} className="btn-primary full-width">
            Back to Dashboard
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
      <div className="app-card fade-in">
        
        <div className="section-header" style={{ flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '4px', marginBottom: '32px' }}>
          <h1 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '32px', textTransform: 'none' }}>Weekly Review</h1>
          <p className="label-text" style={{ textTransform: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', letterSpacing: '0', fontSize: '14px' }}>Log your physical metrics and how you felt this week.</p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
          
          {/* Quantitative Metrics */}
          <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
            <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
              <Scale size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Body Metrics
            </h3>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
              <div>
                <label className="label-text">Current Weight (kg)</label>
                <input name="weight" type="number" step="0.1" required style={inputStyle} placeholder="e.g. 67.5" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="label-text">Waist (cm)</label>
                <input name="waist" type="number" step="0.5" style={inputStyle} placeholder="e.g. 82.0" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Qualitative Feedback */}
          <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
            <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
              <TrendingUp size={18} color="var(--accent-green)" /> Performance & Recovery
            </h3>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
              <div>
                <label className="label-text">Diet Adherence</label>
                <select name="adherence" style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: 'url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=US-ASCII,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%22292.4%22%20height%3D%22292.4%22%3E%3Cpath%20fill%3D%22%23888888%22%20d%3D%22M287%2069.4a17.6%2017.6%200%200%200-13-5.4H18.4c-5%200-9.3%201.8-12.9%205.4A17.6%2017.6%200%200%200%200%2082.2c0%205%201.8%209.3%205.4%2012.9l128%20127.9c3.6%203.6%207.8%205.4%2012.8%205.4s9.2-1.8%2012.8-5.4L287%2095c3.5-3.5%205.4-7.8%205.4-12.8%200-5-1.9-9.2-5.5-12.8z%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E")', backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}>
                  <option value="strict" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Strictly followed the plan</option>
                  <option value="mostly" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Mostly followed, minor slips</option>
                  <option value="struggled" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Struggled this week</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="label-text">Workout Quality (e.g. Core bracing, machine presses)</label>

                  onMouseOut={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'var(--border-subtle)'; e.currentTarget.style.background = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)'; }}
                >
                  <Upload size={24} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ marginBottom: '8px' }} />
                  <span className="label-text">{angle}</span>
                  <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}
          <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>
            {isSubmitting ? 'Saving...' : 'Submit Check-In'}
          </button>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  );

==> src/app/client/messages/page.tsx <==
'use client';
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Send, Paperclip, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MessagesPage() {
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([
    { id: 1, sender: 'coach', text: 'Great job hitting your macro targets yesterday! How did the core warmups feel?', time: '09:41 AM' },
    { id: 2, sender: 'client', text: 'Thanks! The McGill Big 3 felt much better today. Glute bridges are definitely getting easier.', time: '10:15 AM' },
    { id: 3, sender: 'coach', text: 'Perfect. Keep the eccentric portion slow on the machine presses today.', time: '10:20 AM' },
  ]);

  const handleSend = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newMessage.trim()) return;
    
    setMessages([...messages, {
      id: Date.now(),
      sender: 'client',
      text: newMessage,
      time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
    }]);
    setNewMessage('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-[calc(100vh-80px)] md:h-screen max-w-3xl mx-auto border-x border-white/5 relative">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-white/10 p-4 flex items-center gap-3 sticky top-0 z-10">
        <div className="relative">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-blue-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(37,99,235,0.5)]">
            C
          </div>
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full border-2 border-black"></div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-bold text-white drop-shadow-sm">Coach Prudhvi</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-medium">Online</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Message History */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
        {messages.map((msg) => (
          <div key={msg.id} className={`flex flex-col ${msg.sender === 'client' ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'}`}>
            <div className={`max-w-[80%] rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-sm shadow-lg ${
              msg.sender === 'client' 
                ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-tr-sm shadow-[0_4px_15px_rgba(37,99,235,0.3)]' 
                : 'bg-white/10 text-white backdrop-blur-md border border-white/5 rounded-tl-sm'
            }`}>
              {msg.text}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 px-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-white/40">{msg.time}</span>
              {msg.sender === 'client' && <CheckCircle2 size={12} className="text-blue-400" />}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Input Area (Frosted Glass) */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-t border-white/10 pb-safe">
        <form onSubmit={handleSend} className="flex items-center gap-3 relative">
          <button type="button" className="text-white/50 hover:text-white transition-colors p-2">
            <Paperclip size={20} />
          </button>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            value={newMessage}
            onChange={(e) => setNewMessage(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Message your coach..." 
            className="flex-1 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-white rounded-full px-5 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:bg-white/10 transition-all placeholder:text-white/30"
          />
          <button 
            type="submit"
            disabled={!newMessage.trim()}
            className="bg-blue-600 text-white p-3 rounded-full hover:bg-blue-500 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:hover:bg-blue-600 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(37,99,235,0.4)]"
          >
            <Send size={18} className="ml-0.5" />
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
==> src/app/client/page.tsx <==
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { CheckCircle2, Clock } from "lucide-react";

import { GlassCard } from "@/components/GlassCard";
import ClientTrackerDashboard from "@/components/client/ClientTrackerDashboard";
import { dashboardAPI } from "@/services/api";

interface AthleteData {
  todayTraining: any[];

  steps?: number;
  stepGoal?: number;

  sleepHours?: number;
  sleepGoal?: number;

  hydration?: number;
  hydrationGoal?: number;

  workoutCompletion?: number;
  recovery?: number;

  calories?: number;
  workoutsThisWeek?: number;
}

export default function AthleteDashboard() {
  const [athleteData, setAthleteData] = useState<AthleteData | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;

    const fetchDashboard = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await dashboardAPI.getAthleteData();

        if (mounted) {
          setAthleteData(data);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch athlete data:", error);

        /*
         * The tracker can still render while the backend metric endpoint
         * is being connected.
         *
         * This does NOT affect authentication.
         */
        if (mounted) {
          setAthleteData({
            todayTraining: [],
          });
        }
      } finally {
        if (mounted) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    fetchDashboard();

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className="container"
      style={{
        maxWidth: "1180px",
        paddingBottom: "60px",
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        className="section-header"
        style={{
          marginBottom: "30px",
          alignItems: "flex-end",
        }}
      >
        <div>
          <div
            className="label-text"
            style={{
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              marginBottom: "7px",
              letterSpacing: "0.16em",
            }}
          >
            ATHLETE INTELLIGENCE
          </div>

          <h2
            className="animated-gradient-text"
            style={{
              fontSize: "30px",
              textTransform: "none",
              marginBottom: "8px",
            }}
          >
            Your daily pulse
          </h2>

          <span
            className="label-text"
            style={{
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              textTransform: "none",
            }}
          >
            Track movement, recovery and consistency without the noise.
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tracker */}
      <ClientTrackerDashboard
        steps={athleteData?.steps}
        stepGoal={athleteData?.stepGoal}
        sleepHours={athleteData?.sleepHours}
        sleepGoal={athleteData?.sleepGoal}
        hydration={athleteData?.hydration}
        hydrationGoal={athleteData?.hydrationGoal}
        workoutCompletion={athleteData?.workoutCompletion}
        recovery={athleteData?.recovery}
        calories={athleteData?.calories}
        workoutsThisWeek={athleteData?.workoutsThisWeek}
      />

      {/* Today's training */}
      <div
        className="app-card fade-in"
        style={{
          marginTop: "24px",
        }}
      >
        <div
          className="section-header"
          style={{
            marginBottom: "18px",
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <h3
              style={{
                fontSize: "16px",
                marginBottom: "5px",
              }}
            >
              Today's training
            </h3>

            <span
              style={{
                fontSize: "11px",
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              }}
            >
              Your assigned sessions for today.
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: "30px",
              textAlign: "center",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            }}
          >
            Loading training plan...
          </div>
        ) : !athleteData ||
          !athleteData.todayTraining ||
          athleteData.todayTraining.length === 0 ? (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: "24px",
              textAlign: "center",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              borderRadius: "16px",
              background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.025)",
            }}
          >
            No training scheduled for today yet.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="plan-cards-container">
            {athleteData.todayTraining.map((training: any) => (
              <GlassCard
                key={training.id}
                title={training.title}
                subtitle={training.subtitle}
                statusText={training.statusText}
                statusColor={training.statusColor}
                Icon={
                  training.statusText === "completed"
                    ? CheckCircle2
                    : Clock
                }
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
==> src/app/coach/clients/details/page.tsx <==
"use client";
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ArrowLeft, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react';
import { dashboardAPI, CoachClient } from '@/services/api';

// 1. Child component that uses the search params (?id=...)
function ClientDetailsContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const clientId = searchParams.get('id');
  const [client, setClient] = useState<CoachClient | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const load = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await dashboardAPI.getCoachClients();
        setClient(data.clients.find((c) => c.id === clientId) ?? null);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to fetch client:', error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    load();
  }, [clientId]);

  const tile = {
    background: 'var(--bg-input)',
    border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
    padding: '16px',
    display: 'flex',
    flexDirection: 'column' as const,
    gap: '6px',
  };

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
      <div className="app-card fade-in">

        <Link href="/coach/clients" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textDecoration: 'none', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Client Roster
        </Link>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Loading client profile...</div>
        ) : !client ? (
          <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>No athlete found for ID: {clientId ?? '—'}</div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="section-header" style={{ marginBottom: '24px' }}>
              <div>
                <h2 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '28px', textTransform: 'none', marginBottom: '8px' }}>{client.name}</h2>
                <span className="label-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textTransform: 'none' }}>Athlete profile • ID {client.id}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px, 1fr))', gap: '12px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
              <div style={tile}>
                <span className="label-text" style={{ fontSize: '11px' }}>Goal</span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 600 }}>{client.goal}</span>
              </div>
              <div style={tile}>
                <span className="label-text" style={{ fontSize: '11px' }}>Adherence</span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 600, color: client.adherence >= 80 ? 'var(--accent-green)' : 'var(--accent-gold)' }}>{client.adherence}%</span>
              </div>
              <div style={tile}>
                <span className="label-text" style={{ fontSize: '11px' }}>Last check-in</span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 600 }}>{client.lastCheckIn}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Link
              href={`/coach/clients/details/progress?id=${client.id}`}
              className="btn-primary"
              style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px', textDecoration: 'none' }}
            >
              <TrendingUp size={16} /> VIEW PROGRESS ANALYTICS
            </Link>
          </>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// 2. Wrap the child component with Suspense in the default export
export default function ClientDetailsPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div style={{ padding: '24px', color: 'white' }}>Loading client profile...</div>}>
      <ClientDetailsContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

==> src/app/coach/clients/page.tsx <==
"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Search, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { GlassCard } from '@/components/GlassCard';
import { dashboardAPI, CoachClient } from '@/services/api';

export default function CoachClientRoster() {
  const [clients, setClients] = useState<CoachClient[]>([]);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchClients = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await dashboardAPI.getCoachClients();
        setClients(data.clients);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to fetch clients:', error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchClients();
  }, []);

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const visible = clients.filter(
    (c) => !q || c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.goal.toLowerCase().includes(q)
  );

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
      <div className="app-card fade-in">

        <div className="section-header" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
          <div>
            <h2 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '28px', textTransform: 'none', marginBottom: '8px' }}>Client Roster</h2>
            <span className="label-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textTransform: 'none' }}>
              {isLoading ? 'Loading athletes...' : `${clients.length} athletes assigned to you.`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Search */}
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          <Search size={16} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)' }} />
          <input
            type="search"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search by name or goal"
            aria-label="Search clients"
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '14px 14px 14px 40px',
              background: 'var(--bg-input)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              color: 'var(--text-primary)',
              outline: 'none',
              fontSize: '15px',
              boxSizing: 'border-box',
            }}
          />
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Loading Roster...</div>
        ) : visible.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>No athletes match "{query}".</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="plan-cards-container">
            {visible.map((c) => (
              <Link
                key={c.id}
                href={`/coach/clients/details?id=${c.id}`}
                style={{ display: 'block', textDecoration: 'none', color: 'inherit' }}
              >
                <GlassCard
                  title={c.name}
                  subtitle={`${c.goal} • ${c.adherence}% adherence • Check-in: ${c.lastCheckIn}`}
                  statusText={c.status === 'on-track' ? 'on track' : 'needs attention'}
                  statusColor={c.status === 'on-track' ? 'green' : 'red'}
                  Icon={c.status === 'on-track' ? CheckCircle2 : AlertCircle}
                />
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

==> src/app/coach/page.tsx <==
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
} from "lucide-react";

import { GlassCard } from "@/components/GlassCard";
import CoachTrackerDashboard from "@/components/coach/CoachTrackerDashboard";
import { dashboardAPI } from "@/services/api";

interface CoachDashboardData {
  stats: any[];
  recentActivity: any[];
}

/*
 * Read a metric from the backend response.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * There is intentionally NO fallback value here.
 *
 * If the backend has not provided the metric yet, we return
 * undefined so the UI can correctly display "—" / "No data yet"
 * instead of showing fake test numbers.
 */
function getStat(
  stats: any[],
  names: string[]
): number | undefined {
  const match = stats?.find((item) => {
    const name = String(
      item?.name ??
        item?.title ??
        item?.label ??
        ""
    ).toLowerCase();

    return names.some((candidate) =>
      name.includes(candidate.toLowerCase())
    );
  });

  if (!match) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const value = Number(match.value);

  return Number.isFinite(value)
    ? value
    : undefined;
}

export default function CoachDashboard() {
  const [dashboardData, setDashboardData] =
    useState<CoachDashboardData | null>(null);

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;

    const fetchDashboard = async () => {
      try {
        setIsLoading(true);

        const data = await dashboardAPI.getCoachData();

        if (!mounted) return;

        setDashboardData({
          stats: Array.isArray(data?.stats)
            ? data.stats
            : [],

          recentActivity: Array.isArray(
            data?.recentActivity
          )
            ? data.recentActivity
            : [],
        });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to fetch coach data:",
          error
        );

        if (!mounted) return;

        setDashboardData({
          stats: [],
          recentActivity: [],
        });
      } finally {
        if (mounted) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    fetchDashboard();

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const stats = dashboardData?.stats ?? [];

  /*
   * These values come ONLY from the backend.
   *
   * No demo/test numbers are inserted here.
   */
  const activeClients = getStat(
    stats,
    ["active clients"]
  );

  const workoutCompletion = getStat(
    stats,
    ["workout completion"]
  );

  const sleepConsistency = getStat(
    stats,
    ["sleep consistency"]
  );

  const hydration = getStat(
    stats,
    ["hydration"]
  );

  const adherence = getStat(
    stats,
    ["adherence"]
  );

  return (
    <div
      className="container"
      style={{
        maxWidth: "1180px",
        paddingBottom: "60px",
      }}
    >
      {/* =========================================================
          HEADER
         ========================================================= */}

      <div
        className="section-header"
        style={{
          marginBottom: "30px",
        }}
      >
        <div>
          <div
            className="label-text"
            style={{
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              marginBottom: "7px",
              letterSpacing: "0.16em",
            }}
          >
            COACH INTELLIGENCE
          </div>

          <h2
            className="animated-gradient-text"
            style={{
              fontSize: "30px",
              textTransform: "none",
              marginBottom: "8px",
            }}
          >
            Training pulse
          </h2>

          <span
            className="label-text"
            style={{
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              textTransform: "none",
            }}
          >
            A quick view of adherence, recovery and daily
            client activity.
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =========================================================
          LOADING
         ========================================================= */}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div
          className="app-card fade-in"
          style={{
            padding: "45px 20px",
            textAlign: "center",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          Loading coach metrics...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* =====================================================
              TRACKER DASHBOARD

              Undefined values are intentional.
              They mean the backend does not have that metric yet.
             ===================================================== */}

          <CoachTrackerDashboard
            activeClients={activeClients}
            workoutCompletion={workoutCompletion}
            sleepConsistency={sleepConsistency}
            hydration={hydration}
            adherence={adherence}
          />

          {/* =====================================================
              RECENT CLIENT ACTIVITY
             ===================================================== */}

          <section
            className="app-card fade-in"
            style={{
              marginTop: "28px",
            }}
          >
            <div
              className="section-header"
              style={{
                marginBottom: "18px",
              }}
            >
              <div>
                <h3
                  style={{
                    fontSize: "16px",
                    marginBottom: "5px",
                  }}
                >
                  Recent client activity
                </h3>

                <span
                  style={{
                    fontSize: "11px",
                    color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                  }}
                >
                  Recent actions across your training
                  roster.
                </span>
              </div>

              <Activity size={18} />
            </div>

            {dashboardData?.recentActivity?.length ? (
              <div className="plan-cards-container">
                {dashboardData.recentActivity.map(
                  (activity: any) => {
                    const completed =
                      String(activity.status ?? "")
                        .toLowerCase() === "completed";

                    return (
                      <GlassCard
                        key={activity.id}
                        title={
                          activity.client ??
                          "Client"
                        }
                        subtitle={
                          activity.action ??
                          ""
                        }
                        statusText={
                          activity.status ??
                          ""
                        }
                        statusColor={
                          completed
                            ? "green"
                            : "red"
                        }
                        Icon={
                          completed
                            ? CheckCircle2
                            : AlertCircle
                        }
                      />
                    );
                  }
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                style={{
                  padding: "32px 20px",
                  textAlign: "center",
                  color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                  borderRadius: "16px",
                  background:
                    "rgba(255,255,255,0.018)",
                  border:
                    "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.055)",
                }}
              >
                No recent client activity yet.
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Plus, Edit2, Copy, Activity, Calendar, Utensils } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { dashboardAPI } from '@/services/api';

export default function PlanTemplates() {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    setIsCreating(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const created = await dashboardAPI.createTemplate({ title: 'New Template' });
      router.push(`/coach/templates/edit?id=${encodeURIComponent(created.id)}`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Could not create a template.');
      setIsCreating(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTemplates = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await dashboardAPI.getCoachTemplates();
        setTemplates(data.templates);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch templates:", error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchTemplates();
  }, []);

  return (
    <>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
        <div className="app-card fade-in">
          
          {/* Header Row */}
          <div className="section-header" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: '32px' }}>
            <div>
              <h1 style={{ fontSize: '36px', fontWeight: 'bold', margin: '0 0 8px 0', lineHeight: '1.2' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#E5B869' }}>Plan</span><br/>
                <span className="animated-gradient-text">Templates</span>

              <p className="label-text" style={{ textTransform: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', letterSpacing: '0', fontSize: '14px', margin: 0, maxWidth: '240px' }}>
                Build and manage reusable activity and workout frameworks.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={isCreating} 
              className="btn-primary" 
              style={{ padding: '10px 16px', flex: 'none', fontSize: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', background: 'rgba(0, 122, 255, 0.15)', color: '#60A5FA', border: '1px solid rgba(0, 122, 255, 0.4)', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            >
              <Plus size={16} /> {isCreating ? 'CREATING...' : 'NEW TEMPLATE'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}

          {/* Template Cards List */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
            {isLoading ? (
              <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Loading Templates...</div>
