==> src/app/coach/templates/[id]/TemplateClient.tsx <==
"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Save, Plus, GripVertical, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { dashboardAPI } from '@/services/api';

type Exercise = { id: string; name: string; sets: string; reps: string; rpe: string };

const fieldStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)',
  border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
  borderRadius: '8px',
  color: '#fff',
  padding: '8px 10px',
  fontSize: '13px',
  outline: 'none',
  minWidth: 0,
};

export default function TemplateClient() {
  // Works for both /coach/templates/edit?id=... (works in the static app) and the older /coach/templates/[id]
  const params = useParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const search = useSearchParams();
  const templateId = search.get('id') || params?.id || '';

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ type: 'ok' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!templateId) {
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'No template selected.' });
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const t = await dashboardAPI.getTemplate(templateId);
        if (cancelled) return;
        setTitle(t.title || '');
        setExercises(
          (t.exercises || []).map((e: any, i: number) => ({
            id: String(e.id || i + 1),
            name: e.name || '',
            sets: String(e.sets ?? ''),
            reps: String(e.reps ?? ''),
            rpe: String(e.rpe ?? ''),
          }))
        );
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (!cancelled) setMessage({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Could not load this template.' });
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [templateId]);

  const updateExercise = (id: string, patch: Partial<Exercise>) =>
    setExercises((list) => list.map((e) => (e.id === id ? { ...e, ...patch } : e)));

  const addExercise = () =>
    setExercises((list) => [...list, { id: String(Date.now()), name: '', sets: '3', reps: '8-10', rpe: '7' }]);

  const removeExercise = (id: string) => setExercises((list) => list.filter((e) => e.id !== id));

  const save = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      await dashboardAPI.updateTemplate(templateId, {
        title,
        exercises: exercises.filter((e) => e.name.trim()),
      });
      setMessage({ type: 'ok', text: 'Template saved.' });
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Could not save this template.' });
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '800px', paddingBottom: '80px' }}>

      {/* Top Navigation */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
        <Link href="/coach/templates" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Back to Templates
        </Link>
        <button
          onClick={save}
          disabled={isSaving || isLoading || !templateId}
          className="btn-primary"
          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '8px 16px', fontSize: '13px', background: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.15)', color: '#00E27B', border: '1px solid rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.4)' }}
        >
          <Save size={16} /> {isSaving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="app-card fade-in">
        <h1 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '24px', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>Template Configuration</h1>

        {message && (
          <div
            className={message.type === 'error' ? 'error-banner' : undefined}
            style={message.type === 'ok' ? { color: '#00E27B', marginBottom: '16px', fontSize: '14px' } : undefined}
          >
            {message.text}
          </div>
        )}

        {isLoading ? (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Loading Configuration...</div>
        ) : !templateId || (message?.type === 'error' && exercises.length === 0 && !title) ? null : (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px', marginTop: '16px' }}>

            <input
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              maxLength={100}
              placeholder="Template name"
              aria-label="Template name"
              style={{ ...fieldStyle, fontSize: '16px', padding: '12px 14px' }}
            />

            {exercises.map((ex, index) => (
              <div key={ex.id} style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '12px',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                background: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)',
                border: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)',
                padding: '16px',
                borderRadius: '12px'
              }}>
                <GripVertical size={20} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ cursor: 'grab' }} />
                <div style={{ flex: '1 1 200px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <input
                    value={ex.name}
                    onChange={(e) => updateExercise(ex.id, { name: e.target.value })}
                    maxLength={100}
                    placeholder={`${index + 1}. Exercise name`}
                    aria-label={`Exercise ${index + 1} name`}
                    style={fieldStyle}
                  />
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                    <input value={ex.sets} onChange={(e) => updateExercise(ex.id, { sets: e.target.value })} maxLength={10} placeholder="Sets" aria-label="Sets" style={{ ...fieldStyle, width: '33%' }} />
                    <input value={ex.reps} onChange={(e) => updateExercise(ex.id, { reps: e.target.value })} maxLength={10} placeholder="Reps" aria-label="Reps" style={{ ...fieldStyle, width: '33%' }} />
                    <input value={ex.rpe} onChange={(e) => updateExercise(ex.id, { rpe: e.target.value })} maxLength={10} placeholder="RPE" aria-label="RPE" style={{ ...fieldStyle, width: '33%' }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => removeExercise(ex.id)}
                  aria-label={`Remove exercise ${index + 1}`}
                  style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: '#FF453A', cursor: 'pointer', padding: '8px' }}
                >
                  <Trash2 size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}

            <button
              onClick={addExercise}
              style={{
                width: '100%',

              }}
            >

          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

==> src/app/layout.tsx <==
"use client";

import "./globals.css";
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  Activity,
  CalendarCheck,
  Dumbbell,
  LayoutDashboard,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  MessageSquare,
  Settings,
  UserPlus,
  Users,
  Utensils,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { authSession, UserSession } from "@/services/api";

type NavItem = {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  Icon: React.ElementType;
};

const NAVIGATION: Record<"ADMIN" | "COACH" | "CLIENT", NavItem[]> = {
  ADMIN: [
    { href: "/admin", label: "Dashboard", Icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { href: "/admin/onboarding", label: "Onboarding", Icon: UserPlus },
  ],
  COACH: [
    { href: "/coach", label: "Dashboard", Icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { href: "/coach/clients", label: "Client Roster", Icon: Users },
    { href: "/coach/templates", label: "Plan Templates", Icon: Activity },
  ],
  CLIENT: [
    { href: "/client", label: "Today", Icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { href: "/client/diet", label: "Diet Plan", Icon: Utensils },
    { href: "/client/check-in", label: "Check-In", Icon: CalendarCheck },
    { href: "/client/messages", label: "Messages", Icon: MessageSquare },
  ],
};

const PORTAL_LABEL: Record<"ADMIN" | "COACH" | "CLIENT", string> = {
  ADMIN: "MASTER ADMIN",
  COACH: "COACH PORTAL",
  CLIENT: "ATHLETE PORTAL",
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [session, setSession] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const menuOpenRef = useRef(false);
  const navRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const navButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    menuOpenRef.current = isMenuOpen;
  }, [isMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const syncAuth = () => {
      let current = authSession.get();

      if (current && !current.token) {
        authSession.clear();
        current = null;
      }

      setSession(current);

      if (pathname === "/") return;

      if (!current) {
        router.replace("/");
        return;
      }

      const home =
        current.role === "ADMIN"
          ? "/admin"
          : current.role === "COACH"
            ? "/coach"
            : "/client";

      const area = pathname.startsWith("/admin")
        ? "ADMIN"
        : pathname.startsWith("/coach")
          ? "COACH"
          : pathname.startsWith("/client")
            ? "CLIENT"
            : null;

      if (area && area !== current.role) {
        router.replace(home);
      }
    };

    syncAuth();
    window.addEventListener("bluefit:auth-change", syncAuth);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("bluefit:auth-change", syncAuth);
  }, [pathname, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
  }, [pathname]);

  // Left-edge swipe opens the one unified navigation.
  // Right swipe closes it. There is deliberately no right-side navigation gesture.
  useEffect(() => {
    const EDGE = 32;
    const MIN_DX = 60;
    const RATIO = 1.5;
    const MAX_MS = 900;
    let start: { x: number; y: number; t: number } | null = null;

    const ignored = (target: EventTarget | null) =>
      !!(target as HTMLElement | null)?.closest?.(
        'input, textarea, select, [contenteditable="true"], [data-no-swipe]'
      );

    const begin = (x: number, y: number, target: EventTarget | null) => {
      start = ignored(target) ? null : { x, y, t: Date.now() };
    };

    const finish = (x: number, y: number) => {
      if (!start) return;

      const { x: sx, y: sy, t } = start;
      start = null;

      const dx = x - sx;
      const dy = y - sy;
      const duration = Date.now() - t;

      if (
        Math.abs(dx) < MIN_DX ||
        Math.abs(dx) < Math.abs(dy) * RATIO ||
        duration > MAX_MS
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (dx > 0 && !menuOpenRef.current && sx <= EDGE) {
        setIsMenuOpen(true);
      } else if (dx < 0 && menuOpenRef.current) {
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const onTouchStart = (event: TouchEvent) =>
      begin(event.touches[0].clientX, event.touches[0].clientY, event.target);
    const onTouchEnd = (event: TouchEvent) =>
      finish(event.changedTouches[0].clientX, event.changedTouches[0].clientY);

    const onMouseDown = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (event.button === 0) {
        begin(event.clientX, event.clientY, event.target);
      }
    };

    const onMouseUp = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (window.getSelection()?.toString()) {
        start = null;
        return;
      }
      finish(event.clientX, event.clientY);
    };

    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") setIsMenuOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener("touchstart", onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("touchend", onTouchEnd, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
    document.addEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("touchstart", onTouchStart);
      document.removeEventListener("touchend", onTouchEnd);
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
      document.removeEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, []);

  // The menu is a floating right-side stack, not a drawer. Clicking outside
  // the stack closes it without darkening or shifting the dashboard.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMenuOpen) return;

    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const target = event.target as Node | null;
      if (!target) return;

      if (navRef.current?.contains(target)) return;
      if (navButtonRef.current?.contains(target)) return;

      setIsMenuOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    };
  }, [isMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let handle: { remove: () => Promise<void> } | undefined;
    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      try {
        const { App } = await import("@capacitor/app");
        const listener = await App.addListener("backButton", ({ canGoBack }) => {
          if (menuOpenRef.current) {
            setIsMenuOpen(false);
            return;
          }

          if (canGoBack) window.history.back();
          else App.exitApp();
        });

        if (cancelled) {
          await listener.remove();
        } else {
          handle = listener;
        }
      } catch {
        // Browser environment or Capacitor App plugin unavailable.
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      handle?.remove();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    authSession.clear();
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
    router.replace("/");
  };

  if (pathname === "/") {
    return (
      <html lang="en" className="dark">
        <head>
          <meta
            name="viewport"
            content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover"
          />
        </head>
        <body>
          <main className="main-content" style={{ width: "100%" }}>
            {children}
          </main>
        </body>
      </html>
    );
  }

  const role = session?.role;
  const items = role ? NAVIGATION[role] : [];
  const portalLabel = role ? PORTAL_LABEL[role] : "THE BLUE FIT";

  return (
    <html lang="en" className="dark">
      <head>
        <meta
          name="viewport"
          content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover"
        />
      </head>
      <body>
        <div className="app-container">
          <header className="mobile-header">
            <span className="glazy-title" style={{ fontSize: "18px" }}>
              The Blue Fit{session?.userCode ? ` • ${session.userCode}` : ""}
            </span>
          </header>

          {/* The only global navigation trigger. */}
          <button
            ref={navButtonRef}
            className={`nav-fab nav-fab-left ${isMenuOpen ? "active" : ""}`}
            aria-label={isMenuOpen ? "Close navigation" : "Open navigation"}
            aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
            aria-controls="bluefit-navigation"
            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
          >
            {isMenuOpen ? (
              <X size={24} color="#fff" />
            ) : (
              <Menu size={24} color="#fff" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Floating navigation stack. It pops out from the left-side hamburger
              using the compact button treatment from the original reference UI. */}
          <nav
            ref={navRef}
            id="bluefit-navigation"
            className={`bluefit-pop-nav ${isMenuOpen ? "open" : ""} role-${role?.toLowerCase() ?? "client"}`}
            aria-label="Primary navigation"
            aria-hidden={!isMenuOpen}
          >
            {items.map(({ href, label, Icon }, index) => {
              const current =
                pathname === href ||
                (href !== "/admin" &&
                  href !== "/coach" &&
                  href !== "/client" &&
                  pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`));

              return (
                <Link
                  key={href}
                  href={href}
                  aria-current={current ? "page" : undefined}
                  onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)}
                  className={`bluefit-pop-nav-item ${current ? "current" : ""}`}
                  style={{ "--nav-i": index } as React.CSSProperties}
                >
                  <Icon size={21} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                  <span>{label}</span>
                  <span className="bluefit-pop-nav-indicator" aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
              );
            })}

            <button
              type="button"
              className="bluefit-pop-nav-signout"
              onClick={handleLogout}
            >
              <LogOut size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>Sign Out</span>
            </button>
          </nav>

          <main className="main-content">{children}</main>
        </div>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

//                             style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
//                           >
//                             <option value="sedentary" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Sedentary (Desk job, minimal movement)</option>

//                       onClick={() => setActiveDay(day)}
//                       style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1 }}
//                     >
//                       {day}
//                     </button>
//                   ))}
//                 </div>

//                 <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>

//                     </h3>
//                   </div>

//                     {meals.map((meal) => (
//                       <label
//                         key={`${activeDay}-${meal}`}
//                         style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '140px', border: '2px dashed var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)', cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'all 0.2s ease', textAlign: 'center', padding: '12px' }}
//                       >
//                         <Upload size={24} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ marginBottom: '12px' }} />

//                 <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>

//                     Back

// ====================

"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Camera, Upload, Activity, Scale, Briefcase, Dumbbell, Lock, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { dashboardAPI, UserSession } from '@/services/api';

export default function OpeningPortalPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  // Opening screen role toggle
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<'CLIENT' | 'COACH' | 'ADMIN'>('CLIENT');
  const [identifier, setIdentifier] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // First-time login state (Step 1: Set New Password -> Step 2: Metrics -> Step 3: 3-Day Log)
  const [pendingUser, setPendingUser] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1);
  const [activeDay, setActiveDay] = useState('Day 1');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');

  // Coach Onboarding Fields
  const [coachSpecialization, setCoachSpecialization] = useState('hypertrophy');

  // Client Onboarding Fields (Matching your ProvisioningForm)
  const [age, setAge] = useState('');
  const [height, setHeight] = useState('');
  const [weight, setWeight] = useState('');
  const [activityLevel, setActivityLevel] = useState('sedentary');
  const [primaryGoal, setPrimaryGoal] = useState('fat_loss');
  const [medicalNotes, setMedicalNotes] = useState('');

  const inputStyle = {
    width: '100%',
    padding: '14px',
    background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)',
    border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)',
    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
    outline: 'none',
    fontSize: '15px',
    marginTop: '8px',
    transition: 'border-color 0.2s ease'
  };

  const selectBg = 'url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=US-ASCII,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%22292.4%22%20height%3D%22292.4%22%3E%3Cpath%20fill%3D%22%23888888%22%20d%3D%22M287%2069.4a17.6%2017.6%200%200%200-13-5.4H18.4c-5%200-9.3%201.8-12.9%205.4A17.6%2017.6%200%200%200%200%2082.2c0%205%201.8%209.3%205.4%2012.9l128%20127.9c3.6%203.6%207.8%205.4%2012.8%205.4s9.2-1.8%2012.8-5.4L287%2095c3.5-3.5%205.4-7.8%205.4-12.8%200-5-1.9-9.2-5.5-12.8z%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E")';

  const days = ['Day 1', 'Day 2', 'Day 3'];
  const meals = ['Breakfast', 'Brunch', 'Lunch', 'Snacks', 'Dinner'];

  const routeByRole = (role: 'CLIENT' | 'COACH' | 'ADMIN') => {
    if (role === 'CLIENT') router.push('/client');
    else if (role === 'COACH') router.push('/coach');
    else router.push('/admin');
  };

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const user = await dashboardAPI.login(selectedRole, identifier, password);
      if (user.mustChangePassword || user.onboardingCompleted === false) {
        setPendingUser(user);
        setCurrentStep(1);
      } else {
        routeByRole(user.role);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Invalid ID or Password.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePasswordStep = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    if (newPassword.length < 8 || !/[A-Za-z]/.test(newPassword) || !/\d/.test(newPassword)) {
      setError('Use at least 8 characters, with both letters and numbers.');
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setError('Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword === password) {
      setError('New password must be different from your temporary password.');
      return;
    }
    setCurrentStep(2);
  };

  const handleFinalSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!pendingUser) return;
    setError(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const extraPayload = pendingUser.role === 'COACH'
        ? { specialization: coachSpecialization }
        : {
            age: Number(age),
            height: Number(height),
            weight: Number(weight),
            activityLevel,
            primaryGoal,
            medicalNotes,
          };

      const updated = await dashboardAPI.completeFirstTimeSetup({
        userId: pendingUser.PK,
        currentPassword: password,
        newPassword,
        ...extraPayload,
      });

      routeByRole(updated.role);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to complete onboarding.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: pendingUser ? '800px' : '500px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
      <div className="app-card fade-in">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="section-header" style={{ flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: pendingUser ? 'flex-start' : 'center', gap: '4px', marginBottom: '32px', textAlign: pendingUser ? 'left' : 'center' }}>
          <h1 className="animated-gradient-text" style={{ fontSize: '32px', textTransform: 'none' }}>
            {pendingUser ? `Welcome, ${pendingUser.name} (${pendingUser.userCode})` : 'The Blue Fit'}
          </h1>
          <p className="label-text" style={{ textTransform: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', letterSpacing: '0', fontSize: '14px' }}>
            {pendingUser
              ? 'Set your personal password and complete your initial baseline profile.'
              : 'Select your portal and sign in with your Admin-issued ID & password.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* ==================== OPENING LOGIN VIEW ==================== */}
        {!pendingUser && (
          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
            <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={selectedRole === 'CLIENT' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}
                onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('CLIENT'); setError(null); }}
                style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '14px' }}
              >
                <Activity size={16} /> Athlete / Client
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={selectedRole === 'COACH' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}
                onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('COACH'); setError(null); }}
                style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '14px' }}
              >
                <Dumbbell size={16} /> Coach / Trainer
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={selectedRole === 'ADMIN' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}
                onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('ADMIN'); setError(null); }}
                style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '14px' }}
              >
                <Lock size={16} /> Admin
              </button>
            </div>

            <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
              <div>
                <label className="label-text">
                  {selectedRole === 'CLIENT'
                    ? 'Athlete User ID (e.g. CLS001)'
                    : selectedRole === 'COACH'
                      ? 'Coach User ID (e.g. CHR001)'
                      : 'Admin User ID (e.g. ADH001)'}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={identifier}
                  onChange={(e) => setIdentifier(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                  placeholder={
                    selectedRole === 'CLIENT'
                      ? 'CLS001'
                      : selectedRole === 'COACH'
                        ? 'CHR001'
                        : 'ADH001'
                  }
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="label-text">Password</label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                  placeholder="Enter password (or Admin temp password)"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}

            <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
              {isSubmitting
                ? 'Verifying...'
                : `Enter ${
                    selectedRole === 'CLIENT'
                      ? 'Athlete'
                      : selectedRole === 'COACH'
                        ? 'Coach'
                        : 'Admin'
                  } Portal`}
              <ArrowRight size={16} />
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* ==================== FIRST-TIME SETUP & ONBOARDING ==================== */}
        {pendingUser && (
          <>
            <div className="progress-container">
              <div className="progress-line">
                <div
                  className="progress-line-fill"
                  style={{
                    width: currentStep === 1 ? '33%' : currentStep === 2 ? '66%' : '100%'
                  }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="progress-step">
                <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active' : ''}`} />
                <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Password</span>
              </div>
              <div className="progress-step">
                <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active' : ''}`} />
                <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Metrics</span>
              </div>
              {pendingUser.role === 'CLIENT' && (
                <div className="progress-step">
                  <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 3 ? 'active' : ''}`} />
                  <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 3 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>3-Day Log</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* STEP 1: Change Temporary Password */}
            {currentStep === 1 && (
              <form onSubmit={handlePasswordStep} className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
                <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                  <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                    <Lock size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Set Your New Password
                  </h3>
                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                    <div>
                      <label className="label-text">New Password</label>
                      <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={newPassword}
                        onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                        style={inputStyle}
                        placeholder="Minimum 6 characters"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="label-text">Confirm New Password</label>
                      <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={confirmPassword}
                        onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                        style={inputStyle}
                        placeholder="Re-enter new password"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}

                <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">
                  Continue to Initial Onboarding
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            {/* STEP 2: Coach Profile OR Client Physical & Lifestyle Metrics */}
            {currentStep === 2 && (
              <form onSubmit={handleFinalSubmit} className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
                {pendingUser.role === 'COACH' ? (
                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                    <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                      <Briefcase size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Coach Profile
                    </h3>
                    <div>
                      <label className="label-text">Primary Specialization</label>
                      <select
                        value={coachSpecialization}
                        onChange={(e) => setCoachSpecialization(e.target.value)}
                        style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                      >
                        <option value="hypertrophy" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Muscle Hypertrophy & Bodybuilding</option>
                        <option value="nutrition" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Clinical Nutrition & Fat Loss</option>
                        <option value="mobility" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Mobility & Injury Rehab</option>
                        <option value="powerlifting" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Strength & Powerlifting</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: '24px', width: '100%' }}>
                      {isSubmitting ? 'Saving...' : 'Complete Setup & Enter Coach Portal'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                      <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                        <Scale size={18} color="var(--accent-gold)" /> Physical Attributes
                      </h3>
                      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Age</label>
                          <input type="number" required value={age} onChange={(e) => setAge(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} placeholder="e.g. 28" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Height (cm)</label>
                          <input type="number" step="0.1" required value={height} onChange={(e) => setHeight(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} placeholder="e.g. 175" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Current Weight (kg)</label>
                          <input type="number" step="0.1" required value={weight} onChange={(e) => setWeight(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} placeholder="e.g. 82.5" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                      <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', marginBottom: '20px', fontWeight: '600' }}>
                        <Activity size={18} color="#60A5FA" /> Lifestyle & Health
                      </h3>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Daily Activity Level</label>
                          <select
                            value={activityLevel}
                            onChange={(e) => setActivityLevel(e.target.value)}
                            style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                          >
                            <option value="sedentary" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Sedentary (Desk job, minimal movement)</option>
                            <option value="moderate" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Moderately Active (3-5 workouts/week)</option>
                            <option value="high" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Very Active (Heavy training / active work)</option>
                          </select>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Primary Fitness Goal</label>
                          <select
                            value={primaryGoal}
                            onChange={(e) => setPrimaryGoal(e.target.value)}
                            style={{...inputStyle, WebkitAppearance: 'none', backgroundImage: selectBg, backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat', backgroundPosition: 'right 14px top 50%', backgroundSize: '10px auto'}}
                          >
                            <option value="fat_loss" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Fat Loss & Definition</option>
                            <option value="hypertrophy" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Muscle Hypertrophy</option>
                            <option value="recomp" style={{ background: '#111' }}>Body Recomposition</option>
                          </select>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="label-text">Medical Notes / Limitations</label>
                          <textarea
                            rows={3}
                            value={medicalNotes}
                            onChange={(e) => setMedicalNotes(e.target.value)}
                            style={{ ...inputStyle, resize: 'none' }}
                            placeholder="List any past injuries or dietary restrictions..."
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={!age || !height || !weight}
                      onClick={() => setCurrentStep(3)}
                      className="btn-primary"
                    >
                      Continue to 3-Day Food Log
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}
              </form>
            )}

            {/* STEP 3: 3-Day Diet Recall (Clients Only) */}
            {currentStep === 3 && pendingUser.role === 'CLIENT' && (
              <form onSubmit={handleFinalSubmit} className="fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
                <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
                  {days.map((day) => (
                    <button
                      key={day}
                      type="button"
                      className={activeDay === day ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}
                      onClick={() => setActiveDay(day)}
                      style={{ padding: '12px', flex: 1 }}
                    >
                      {day}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-input)', border: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)', padding: '24px', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                    <h3 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '600', margin: 0 }}>
                      <Camera size={18} color="#60A5FA" /> {activeDay} Dietary Recall
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
                    {meals.map((meal) => (
                      <label
                        key={`${activeDay}-${meal}`}
                        style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '140px', border: '2px dashed var(--border-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)', cursor: 'pointer', transition: 'all 0.2s ease', textAlign: 'center', padding: '12px' }}
                      >
                        <Upload size={24} color="var(--text-secondary)" style={{ marginBottom: '12px' }} />
                        <span className="label-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginBottom: '4px' }}>{meal}</span>
                        <input type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                      </label>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}

                <div className="button-row" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setCurrentStep(2)} className="btn-secondary">
                    Back
                  </button>
                  <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="btn-primary">
                    {isSubmitting ? 'Saving Assessment...' : 'Save Assessment & Enter Athlete Portal'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
==> src/components/client/ClientTrackerDashboard.tsx <==
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  Droplets,
  Flame,
  Footprints,
  HeartPulse,
  Moon,
  Trophy,
  Dumbbell,
  ChevronRight,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";

import MetricCard from "@/components/shared/MetricCard";
import ProgressRing from "@/components/shared/ProgressRing";

interface ClientTrackerDashboardProps {
  steps?: number;
  stepGoal?: number;
  sleepHours?: number;
  sleepGoal?: number;
  hydration?: number;
  hydrationGoal?: number;
  workoutCompletion?: number;
  recovery?: number;
  calories?: number;
  workoutsThisWeek?: number;
}

const clamp = (value: number) => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(value) || 0));

function ConcentricRings({
  steps,
  sleep,
  activity,
}: {
  steps?: number;
  sleep?: number;
  activity?: number;
}) {
  const rawValues: Array<number | undefined> = [steps, sleep, activity];
  const colors = ["#68C7A1", "#8E82C7", "#C19A5A"];
  const rings = [
    { radius: 82, width: 9 },
    { radius: 65, width: 8 },
    { radius: 48, width: 8 },
  ];

  const [animated, setAnimated] = useState([0, 0, 0]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0;
    const start = performance.now();
    const duration = 950;

    setAnimated([0, 0, 0]);

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const ratio = Math.min((time - start) / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - ratio, 3);
      setAnimated(
        rawValues.map((value) =>
          value === undefined ? 0 : clamp(value) * eased
        )
      );
      if (ratio < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [steps, sleep, activity]);

  const hasAnyData = rawValues.some((value) => value !== undefined);
  const size = 205;
  const center = size / 2;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        position: "relative",
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}
      aria-label="Daily movement, sleep and training progress"
    >
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        style={{ overflow: "visible", transform: "rotate(-90deg)" }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        {rings.map((ring, index) => {
          const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * ring.radius;
          const dashOffset =
            circumference - (animated[index] / 100) * circumference;
          const hasValue = rawValues[index] !== undefined;

          return (
            <g key={ring.radius}>
              <circle
                cx={center}
                cy={center}
                r={ring.radius}
                fill="none"
                stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.045)"
                strokeWidth={ring.width}
              />

              {hasValue && (
                <circle
                  cx={center}
                  cy={center}
                  r={ring.radius}
                  fill="none"
                  stroke={colors[index]}
                  strokeWidth={ring.width}
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeDasharray={circumference}
                  strokeDashoffset={dashOffset}
                  style={{
                    transition: "stroke-dashoffset 70ms linear",
                    filter: `drop-shadow(0 0 4px ${colors[index]}2b)`,
                  }}
                />
              )}
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>

      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          flexDirection: "column",
          textAlign: "center",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: "31px",
            fontWeight: 700,
            lineHeight: 1,
            letterSpacing: "-0.05em",
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
          }}
        >
          {hasAnyData ? `${Math.round(animated[2])}%` : "—"}
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: "6px",
            fontSize: "9px",
            letterSpacing: "0.16em",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          {hasAnyData ? "Daily pulse" : "No activity yet"}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PulseLegend({
  color,
  title,
  value,
  detail,
}: {
  color: string;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  detail: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns: "10px minmax(0,1fr) auto",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: "10px",
      }}
    >
      <span
        style={{
          width: "7px",
          height: "7px",
          borderRadius: "999px",
          background: color,
          opacity: value === "—" ? 0.25 : 1,
          boxShadow: `0 0 7px ${color}44`,
        }}
      />

      <div>
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: "10px",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            letterSpacing: "0.09em",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </div>
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: "3px",
            fontSize: "11px",
            color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.46)",
          }}
        >
          {detail}
        </div>
      </div>

      <strong
        style={{
          fontSize: "13px",
          fontWeight: 600,
          color: value === "—" ? "var(--text-secondary)" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)",
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </strong>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ClientTrackerDashboard({
  steps,
  stepGoal,
  sleepHours,
  sleepGoal,
  hydration,
  hydrationGoal,
  workoutCompletion,
  recovery,
  calories,
  workoutsThisWeek,
}: ClientTrackerDashboardProps) {
  const stepProgress =
    steps !== undefined && stepGoal !== undefined && stepGoal > 0
      ? clamp((steps / stepGoal) * 100)
      : undefined;

  const sleepProgress =
    sleepHours !== undefined && sleepGoal !== undefined && sleepGoal > 0
      ? clamp((sleepHours / sleepGoal) * 100)
      : undefined;

  const hydrationProgress =
    hydration !== undefined &&
    hydrationGoal !== undefined &&
    hydrationGoal > 0
      ? clamp((hydration / hydrationGoal) * 100)
      : undefined;

  const hasAnyData =
    steps !== undefined ||
    sleepHours !== undefined ||
    hydration !== undefined ||
    workoutCompletion !== undefined ||
    recovery !== undefined ||
    calories !== undefined ||
    workoutsThisWeek !== undefined;

  const [weeklyBars, setWeeklyBars] = useState([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
  const hasWeeklyData = workoutsThisWeek !== undefined;

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0;
    const start = performance.now();
    const duration = 850;

    const target = hasWeeklyData
      ? [null, null, null, null, null, null, clamp(workoutCompletion ?? 0)]
      : [null, null, null, null, null, null, null];

    setWeeklyBars([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);

    if (!hasWeeklyData) return () => undefined;

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const ratio = Math.min((time - start) / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - ratio, 3);

      setWeeklyBars(
        target.map((value) =>
          value === null ? 0 : Number(value) * eased
        )
      );

      if (ratio < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [workoutCompletion, workoutsThisWeek, hasWeeklyData]);

  return (
    <section
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        gap: "18px",
      }}
    >
      {/* Primary health overview */}
      <section className="bf-premium-overview">
        <div className="bf-overview-glow" aria-hidden="true" />

        <div className="bf-overview-copy">
          <div className="bf-kicker">HEALTH OVERVIEW</div>

          <h3>Today's rhythm</h3>

          <p>
            {hasAnyData
              ? "Movement, recovery and training — brought together into one quiet daily signal."
              : "Your personal activity will appear here as you train, move, recover and check in."}
          </p>

          <div className="bf-pulse-legend">
            <PulseLegend
              color="#68C7A1"
              title="Movement"
              value={steps !== undefined ? steps.toLocaleString() : "—"}
              detail={
                stepProgress !== undefined && stepGoal !== undefined
                  ? `${Math.round(stepProgress)}% of step goal`
                  : "No activity data yet"
              }
            />

            <PulseLegend
              color="#8E82C7"
              title="Sleep"
              value={sleepHours !== undefined ? `${sleepHours.toFixed(1)}h` : "—"}
              detail={
                sleepProgress !== undefined
                  ? `${Math.round(sleepProgress)}% of target`
                  : "No sleep data yet"
              }
            />

            <PulseLegend
              color="#C19A5A"
              title="Training"
              value={workoutCompletion !== undefined ? `${Math.round(workoutCompletion)}%` : "—"}
              detail={
                workoutCompletion !== undefined
                  ? "weekly completion"
                  : "No workout data yet"
              }
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bf-overview-rings">
          <ConcentricRings
            steps={stepProgress}
            sleep={sleepProgress}
            activity={workoutCompletion}
          />

          <div className="bf-ring-caption">
            <span>Movement</span>
            <span>Sleep</span>
            <span>Training</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Daily focus */}
      <section className="bf-focus-card">
        <div className="bf-focus-icon">
          <HeartPulse size={17} />
        </div>

        <div className="bf-focus-copy">
          <div className="bf-kicker">TODAY'S FOCUS</div>

          <h4>
            {recovery !== undefined ? "Protect the rhythm." : "Build your first signal."}
          </h4>

          <p>
            {recovery !== undefined
              ? `Recovery is at ${Math.round(recovery)}%. Keep hydration moving and complete your planned session.`
              : "Once activity and check-in data are recorded, your recovery and daily focus will appear here."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <ProgressRing
          value={recovery}
          size={90}
          strokeWidth={6}
          label="Recovery"
          accent="#63B99B"
          glow={1}
        />
      </section>

      {/* Core metrics */}
      <section className="bf-client-metric-grid">
        <MetricCard
          title="Steps"
          value={steps !== undefined ? steps.toLocaleString() : "—"}
          description={
            stepGoal !== undefined
              ? `Goal ${stepGoal.toLocaleString()} steps`

==> src/components/client/DailyTask.tsx <==
'use client';
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react';

type TaskProps = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  target: number;
  unit: string;
  type: 'boolean' | 'numeric';
  initialProgress?: number;
};

export default function DailyTask({ id, title, target, unit, type, initialProgress = 0 }: TaskProps) {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(initialProgress);
  const isComplete = progress >= target;

  const handleToggle = () => {
    setProgress(isComplete ? 0 : target);
    // In production: trigger API update to DynamoDB here
  };

  const handleIncrement = (amount: number) => {
    const newVal = Math.min(Math.max(0, progress + amount), target);
    setProgress(newVal);
    // In production: trigger API update to DynamoDB here (ideally debounced)
  };

  return (
    <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all ${isComplete ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'}`}>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h4 className={`font-bold ${isComplete ? 'text-emerald-900 line-through opacity-70' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>{title}</h4>
          <p className={`text-sm ${isComplete ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
            {type === 'numeric' ? `${progress} / ${target} ${unit}` : `${target} ${unit}`}
          </p>
        </div>

        {type === 'boolean' ? (
          <button 
            onClick={handleToggle}
            className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-colors ${
              isComplete ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Check size={20} />
          </button>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={() => handleIncrement(-0.5)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 disabled:opacity-50" disabled={progress <= 0}>
              <Minus size={16} />
            </button>
            <span className="w-8 text-center font-bold text-slate-900">{progress}</span>
            <button onClick={() => handleIncrement(0.5)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-100 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-200 disabled:opacity-50" disabled={isComplete}>
              <Plus size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Progress Bar for Numeric types */}
      {type === 'numeric' && (
        <div className="mt-4 h-2 w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div 
            className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${isComplete ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}`}
            style={{ width: `${(progress / target) * 100}%` }}
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

// --- Neon Icon Components ---
const FlameIcon = () => (
  <svg className="neon-icon neon-orange" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    <path d="M8.5 14.5A2.5 2.5 0 0011 12c0-1.38-.5-2-1-3-1.072-2.143-.224-4.054 2-6 .5 2.5 2 4.9 4 6.5 2 1.6 3 3.5 3 5.5a7 7 0 11-14 0c0-1.153.433-2.294 1-3a2.5 2.5 0 002.5 2.5z"></path>
  </svg>
);

const DumbbellIcon = () => (
  <svg className="neon-icon neon-green" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    <line x1="6" y1="12" x2="18" y2="12"></line>
    <rect x="4" y="9" width="2" height="6" rx="1"></rect>
    <rect x="18" y="9" width="2" height="6" rx="1"></rect>
    <rect x="2" y="10" width="2" height="4" rx="1"></rect>
    <rect x="20" y="10" width="2" height="4" rx="1"></rect>
  </svg>
);

const DietPlanner = () => {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1); 
  
  const [mealsCount, setMealsCount] = useState(1);
  const [dietType, setDietType] = useState('veg');
  const [goal, setGoal] = useState('maintenance');
  const [selectedCalorieTier, setSelectedCalorieTier] = useState(600); 
  
  // FIX: Explicit TypeScript definitions added to state
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<Record<string, string[]>>({});
  const [selectedItems, setSelectedItems] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [openCategory, setOpenCategory] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<any>(null);
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Set NEXT_PUBLIC_DIET_API_URL in your .env file (kept out of source control)
  const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DIET_API_URL ?? '';

  const calorieTiers = [];
  for (let low = 500; low < 3000; low += 100) {
    const high = low + 100;
    const midpoint = low + 50;
    calorieTiers.push({ label: `${low} - ${high} kcal`, value: midpoint });
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchMenu = async () => {
      if (!API_URL) return;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(API_URL, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'get_menu', service: 'Diet' })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (data.status === 'success') {
          setMenu(data.menu);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch menu:", err);
      }
    };
    fetchMenu();
  }, []);

  // FIX: Added 'string' type to item parameter
  const handleCheckboxChange = (item: string) => {
    setSelectedItems(prev => 
      prev.includes(item) 
        ? prev.filter(i => i !== item) 
        : [...prev, item]
    );
  };

  // FIX: Added 'string' type to category parameter
  const toggleCategory = (category: string) => {
    setOpenCategory(prev => (prev === category ? null : category));
  };

  const nextStep = () => {
    setError('');
    setCurrentStep(prev => prev + 1);
  };
  
  const prevStep = () => {
    setError('');
    setCurrentStep(prev => prev - 1);
  };

  const triggerService = async () => {
    if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
      setError('Please select at least one ingredient.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    setResult(null);

    const payload = {
      service: 'Diet',
      action: 'generate_diet',
      preferences: {
        type: dietType,
        mealsCount: Number(mealsCount),
        goal: goal,
        targetCalories: Number(selectedCalorieTier),
        mustInclude: selectedItems
      }
    };

    if (!API_URL) {
      setError('The diet service is not configured yet.');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(API_URL, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || data.error) throw new Error(data.error || 'The request failed.');
      setResult(data);
      setCurrentStep(3); 
    } catch (err: any) { // FIX: Explicitly cast err to 'any'
      setError(err.message || 'An unexpected error occurred');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="App">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="app-card">
          
          {/* Visual Progress Stepper */}
          <div className="progress-container">
            <div className="progress-line">
              <div 
                className="progress-line-fill" 
                style={{ width: currentStep === 1 ? '0%' : currentStep === 2 ? '50%' : '100%' }}
              ></div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="progress-step">
              <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active' : ''}`}></div>
              <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 1 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Goals</span>
            </div>
            <div className="progress-step">
              <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active' : ''}`}></div>
              <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 2 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Ingredients</span>
            </div>
            <div className="progress-step">
              <div className={`progress-dot ${currentStep >= 3 ? 'active' : ''}`}></div>
              <span className={`progress-label ${currentStep >= 3 ? 'active-label' : ''}`}>Plan</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* STEP 1: CONFIG & GOAL */}
          {currentStep === 1 && (
            <div className="step-content fade-in">
              <div className="section-header">
                <span className="step-badge">1</span> 
                <h2 className="glazy-title">Meal Structure & Goals</h2>
              </div>
              
              <div className="form-grid">
                
                {/* Diet Type */}
                <div className="input-group">
                  <label className="label-text">Diet Type</label>
                  <div className={`accordion-item ${openCategory === 'dietType' ? 'open' : ''}`}>
                    <div className="accordion-header" onClick={() => toggleCategory('dietType')}>
                      <h4>{dietType === 'veg' ? 'Vegetarian' : dietType === 'non-veg' ? 'Non-Vegetarian' : 'Snacks Only'}</h4>
                      <span className="chevron">{openCategory === 'dietType' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="accordion-body">
                      <div className="checkbox-grid">
                        {[
                          { label: 'Vegetarian', value: 'veg' },
                          { label: 'Non-Vegetarian', value: 'non-veg' },
                          { label: 'Snacks Only', value: 'snack' }
                        ].map((opt) => (
                          <label 
                            key={opt.value} 
                            className={`custom-checkbox ${dietType === opt.value ? 'selected-item-row' : ''}`}
                          >
                            <input 
                              type="radio" 
                              name="dietType"
                              checked={dietType === opt.value}
                              onChange={() => { setDietType(opt.value); setOpenCategory(null); }}
                            />
                            <span className="checkmark"></span>
                            <span className="checkbox-text">{opt.label}</span>
                          </label>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Number of Meals */}
                <div className="input-group">
                  <label className="label-text">Number of Meals</label>
                  <div className={`accordion-item ${openCategory === 'mealsCount' ? 'open' : ''}`}>
                    <div className="accordion-header" onClick={() => toggleCategory('mealsCount')}>
                      <h4>{mealsCount} {mealsCount === 1 ? 'Meal' : 'Meals'}</h4>
                      <span className="chevron">{openCategory === 'mealsCount' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="accordion-body">
                      <div className="checkbox-grid">
                        {[
                          { label: '1 Meal', value: 1 },
                          { label: '2 Meals', value: 2 },
                          { label: '3 Meals', value: 3 }
                        ].map((opt) => (
                          <label 
                            key={opt.value} 
                            className={`custom-checkbox ${Number(mealsCount) === opt.value ? 'selected-item-row' : ''}`}
                          >
                            <input 
                              type="radio" 
                              name="mealsCount"
                              checked={Number(mealsCount) === opt.value}
                              onChange={() => { setMealsCount(opt.value); setOpenCategory(null); }}
                            />
                            <span className="checkmark"></span>
                            <span className="checkbox-text">{opt.label}</span>
                          </label>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Fitness Goal */}
                <div className="input-group">
                  <label className="label-text">Fitness Goal</label>
                  <div className={`accordion-item ${openCategory === 'goal' ? 'open' : ''}`}>
                    <div className="accordion-header" onClick={() => toggleCategory('goal')}>
                      <h4>{goal === 'fat_loss' ? 'Fat Loss' : goal === 'weight_gain' ? 'Weight Gain' : 'Maintenance'}</h4>
                      <span className="chevron">{openCategory === 'goal' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="accordion-body">
                      <div className="checkbox-grid">
                        {[
                          { label: 'Fat Loss', value: 'fat_loss' },
                          { label: 'Weight Gain', value: 'weight_gain' },
                          { label: 'Maintenance', value: 'maintenance' }
                        ].map((opt) => (
                          <label 
                            key={opt.value} 
                            className={`custom-checkbox ${goal === opt.value ? 'selected-item-row' : ''}`}
                          >
                            <input 
                              type="radio" 
                              name="goal"
                              checked={goal === opt.value}
                              onChange={() => { setGoal(opt.value); setOpenCategory(null); }}
                            />
                            <span className="checkmark"></span>
                            <span className="checkbox-text">{opt.label}</span>
                          </label>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

              </div>

              {/* Calorie Tier */}
              <div className="input-group full-width">
                <label className="label-text">Select Calorie Tier</label>
                <div className={`accordion-item ${openCategory === 'calorieTier' ? 'open' : ''}`}>
                  <div className="accordion-header" onClick={() => toggleCategory('calorieTier')}>
                    <h4>{calorieTiers.find(t => Number(t.value) === Number(selectedCalorieTier))?.label || 'Select Tier'}</h4>
                    <span className="chevron">{openCategory === 'calorieTier' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="accordion-body scrollable-body">
                    <div className="checkbox-grid">
                      {calorieTiers.map((tier) => (
                        <label 
                          key={tier.value} 
                          className={`custom-checkbox ${Number(selectedCalorieTier) === tier.value ? 'selected-item-row' : ''}`}
                        >
                          <input 
                            type="radio" 
                            name="calorieTier"
                            checked={Number(selectedCalorieTier) === tier.value}
                            onChange={() => { setSelectedCalorieTier(tier.value); setOpenCategory(null); }}
                          />
                          <span className="checkmark"></span>
                          <span className="checkbox-text">{tier.label}</span>
                        </label>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button onClick={nextStep} className="btn-primary mt-24">
                Continue
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 2: INGREDIENT SELECTION */}
          {currentStep === 2 && (
            <div className="step-content fade-in">
              <div className="section-header">
                <span className="step-badge">2</span> 
                <h2 className="glazy-title">Select Ingredients</h2>
              </div>
              
              {Object.keys(menu).length === 0 ? (
                <div className="loading-state">Loading database...</div>
              ) : (
                <div className="accordion-container">
                  {Object.entries(menu)
                    .filter(([category]) => {
                      if (dietType === 'veg' && category === 'NonVeg') return false;
                      if (dietType === 'snack' && !['Fruits', 'Seeds'].includes(category)) return false;
                      return true;
                    })
                    .map(([category, items]) => {
                      const isOpen = openCategory === category;
                      
                      const sortedItems = [...items].sort((a, b) => {
                        const aSelected = selectedItems.includes(a);
                        const bSelected = selectedItems.includes(b);
                        if (aSelected && !bSelected) return -1;
                        if (!aSelected && bSelected) return 1;
                        return a.localeCompare(b);
                      });

                      return (
                        <div key={category} className={`accordion-item ${isOpen ? 'open' : ''}`}>
                          <div className="accordion-header" onClick={() => toggleCategory(category)}>
                            <h4>{category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</h4>
                            <span className="chevron">{isOpen ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="accordion-body scrollable-body">
                            <div className="checkbox-grid">
                              {sortedItems.map(item => {
                                const isSelected = selectedItems.includes(item);
                                return (
                                  <label key={item} className={`custom-checkbox ${isSelected ? 'selected-item-row' : ''}`}>
                                    <input 
                                      type="checkbox" 
                                      checked={isSelected}
                                      onChange={() => handleCheckboxChange(item)}
                                    />
                                    <span className="checkmark"></span>
                                    <span className="checkbox-text">{item}</span>
                                  </label>
                                );
                              })}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}

              {error && <div className="error-banner">{error}</div>}

              <div className="button-row">
                <button onClick={prevStep} className="btn-secondary" disabled={loading}>
                  Back
                </button>
                <button onClick={triggerService} disabled={loading} className="btn-primary">
                  {loading ? 'Building...' : 'Generate Plan'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 3: RESULTS SECTION */}
          {currentStep === 3 && result && result.plan && (
            <div className="step-content fade-in">
               <div className="section-header">
                <span className="step-badge">3</span> 
                <h2 className="glazy-title">Your Custom Plan</h2>
              </div>
              
              <div className="results-summary">
                <p className="subtitle">({result.summary.Meals} Meal Split) — {result.summary.Goal}</p>
                <div className="macro-row">
                  <div className="macro-pill">
                    <FlameIcon /> 
                    <strong className="pulse-text">{result.summary['Total Calories']}</strong> kcal
                  </div>
                  <div className="macro-pill">
                    <DumbbellIcon /> 
                    <strong className="pulse-text">{result.summary['Total Protein']}</strong>g Protein
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Modern Plan Cards List */}
              <div className="plan-cards-container">
                {result.plan.map((item: any, idx: number) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="plan-card">
                    <div className="plan-card-left">
                      <span className="plan-item-name">{item.Item}</span>
                      <span className="plan-item-category">{item.Category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="plan-card-right">
                      <span className="plan-item-portion">{item['Portion (g)']}g</span>
                      <div className="plan-item-macros">
                        <span className="macro-stat"><FlameIcon /> {item.Calories} kcal</span>
                        <span className="macro-stat"><DumbbellIcon /> {item.Protein}g</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <button onClick={() => setCurrentStep(1)} className="btn-secondary mt-24 full-width">
                Start Over
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default DietPlanner;
