"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  CheckCircle2,
  Droplets,
  Dumbbell,
  HeartPulse,
  Moon,
  TrendingUp,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";

import MetricCard from "@/components/shared/MetricCard";
import ProgressRing from "@/components/shared/ProgressRing";

interface CoachTrackerDashboardProps {
  activeClients?: number;
  workoutCompletion?: number;
  sleepConsistency?: number;
  hydration?: number;
  adherence?: number;
}

const clamp = (value: number) => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(value) || 0));

function CoachPulseRings({
  adherence,
  workout,
  recovery,
}: {
  adherence?: number;
  workout?: number;
  recovery?: number;
}) {
  const rawValues: Array<number | undefined> = [adherence, workout, recovery];
  const colors = ["#63B99B", "#C19A5A", "#8E82C7"];
  const radii = [78, 60, 43];
  const widths = [9, 8, 8];
  const size = 195;
  const center = size / 2;
  const [animated, setAnimated] = useState([0, 0, 0]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0;
    const start = performance.now();
    const duration = 950;

    setAnimated([0, 0, 0]);

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const ratio = Math.min((time - start) / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - ratio, 3);
      setAnimated(
        rawValues.map((value) =>
          value === undefined ? 0 : clamp(value) * eased
        )
      );
      if (ratio < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [adherence, workout, recovery]);

  const hasAnyData = rawValues.some((value) => value !== undefined);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "relative",
        width: size,
        height: size,
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}
    >
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        style={{ transform: "rotate(-90deg)", overflow: "visible" }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        {radii.map((radius, index) => {
          const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
          const offset =
            circumference - (animated[index] / 100) * circumference;
          const hasValue = rawValues[index] !== undefined;

          return (
            <g key={radius}>
              <circle
                cx={center}
                cy={center}
                r={radius}
                fill="none"
                stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.045)"
                strokeWidth={widths[index]}
              />

              {hasValue && (
                <circle
                  cx={center}
                  cy={center}
                  r={radius}
                  fill="none"
                  stroke={colors[index]}
                  strokeWidth={widths[index]}
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeDasharray={circumference}
                  strokeDashoffset={offset}
                  style={{
                    transition: "stroke-dashoffset 70ms linear",
                    filter: `drop-shadow(0 0 4px ${colors[index]}22)`,
                  }}
                />
              )}
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>

      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          textAlign: "center",
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: "30px",
            fontWeight: 700,
            lineHeight: 1,
            letterSpacing: "-0.05em",
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
          }}
        >
          {hasAnyData ? `${Math.round(animated[0])}%` : "—"}
        </span>

        <span
          style={{
            marginTop: "6px",
            fontSize: "9px",
            letterSpacing: "0.15em",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          {hasAnyData ? "Client pulse" : "No client data"}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CoachLegend({
  color,
  title,
  value,
  detail,
}: {
  color: string;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  detail: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns: "10px minmax(0,1fr) auto",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: "10px",
      }}
    >
      <span
        style={{
          width: "7px",
          height: "7px",
          borderRadius: "999px",
          background: color,
          opacity: value === "—" ? 0.25 : 1,
          boxShadow: `0 0 7px ${color}33`,
        }}
      />

      <div>
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: "10px",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            letterSpacing: "0.09em",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: "3px",
            fontSize: "11px",
            color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.46)",
          }}
        >
          {detail}
        </div>
      </div>

      <strong
        style={{
          fontSize: "13px",
          fontWeight: 600,
          color:
            value === "—"
              ? "var(--text-secondary)"
              : "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)",
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </strong>
    </div>
  );
}

function Signal({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  value,
  accent,
}: {
  icon: React.ElementType;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  accent: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bf-signal">
      <div
        className="bf-signal-icon"
        style={{
          color: accent,
          background: `${accent}0f`,
          borderColor: `${accent}18`,
        }}
      >
        <Icon size={15} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="bf-signal-title">{title}</div>
        <div className="bf-signal-value">{value}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CoachTrackerDashboard({
  activeClients,
  workoutCompletion,
  sleepConsistency,
  hydration,
  adherence,
}: CoachTrackerDashboardProps) {
  const hasAnyData =
    activeClients !== undefined ||
    workoutCompletion !== undefined ||
    sleepConsistency !== undefined ||
    hydration !== undefined ||
    adherence !== undefined;

  const hasWeeklyData =
    adherence !== undefined || workoutCompletion !== undefined;

  const [weeklyBars, setWeeklyBars] = useState([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasWeeklyData) {
      setWeeklyBars([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
      return () => undefined;
    }

    let frame = 0;
    const start = performance.now();
    const duration = 850;
    const finalValue = clamp(adherence ?? workoutCompletion ?? 0);

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const ratio = Math.min((time - start) / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - ratio, 3);
      setWeeklyBars([
        0,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        finalValue * eased,
      ]);
      if (ratio < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    setWeeklyBars([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [adherence, workoutCompletion, hasWeeklyData]);

  return (
    <section className="bf-coach-tracker">
      {/* Main client pulse */}
      <section className="bf-coach-overview">
        <div className="bf-coach-overview-glow" aria-hidden="true" />

        <div className="bf-coach-copy">
          <div className="bf-kicker">CLIENT HEALTH OVERVIEW</div>
          <h3>Training pulse.</h3>
          <p>
            {hasAnyData
              ? "One calm view of adherence, training, recovery and hydration across your current roster."
              : "Client activity will appear here as athletes are assigned and begin recording their progress."}
          </p>

          <div className="bf-coach-legend">
            <CoachLegend
              color="#63B99B"
              title="Adherence"
              value={adherence !== undefined ? `${Math.round(adherence)}%` : "—"}
              detail={adherence !== undefined ? "weekly rhythm" : "No data yet"}
            />

            <CoachLegend
              color="#C19A5A"
              title="Training"
              value={
                workoutCompletion !== undefined
                  ? `${Math.round(workoutCompletion)}%`
                  : "—"
              }
              detail={
                workoutCompletion !== undefined
                  ? "sessions completed"
                  : "No data yet"
              }
            />

            <CoachLegend
              color="#8E82C7"
              title="Recovery"
              value={
                sleepConsistency !== undefined
                  ? `${Math.round(sleepConsistency)}%`
                  : "—"
              }
              detail={
                sleepConsistency !== undefined
                  ? "sleep consistency"
                  : "No data yet"
              }
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bf-coach-rings">
          <CoachPulseRings
            adherence={adherence}
            workout={workoutCompletion}
            recovery={sleepConsistency}
          />

          <div className="bf-coach-ring-caption">
            <span>Adherence</span>
            <span>Training</span>
            <span>Recovery</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Metrics */}
      <section className="bf-coach-metric-grid">
        <MetricCard
          title="Active clients"
          value={activeClients !== undefined ? activeClients : "—"}
          description={
            activeClients !== undefined
              ? "currently training"
              : "No clients assigned"
          }
          accent="#68AFC3"
          icon={<Users size={18} />}
        />

        <MetricCard
          title="Workout completion"
          value={
            workoutCompletion !== undefined
              ? Math.round(clamp(workoutCompletion))
              : "—"
          }
          unit={workoutCompletion !== undefined ? "%" : ""}
          description={
            workoutCompletion !== undefined
              ? "scheduled sessions completed"
              : "No workout data yet"
          }
          progress={workoutCompletion}
          progressLabel="Completion"
          accent="#B89257"
          icon={<Dumbbell size={18} />}
        />

        <MetricCard
          title="Sleep consistency"
          value={
            sleepConsistency !== undefined
              ? Math.round(clamp(sleepConsistency))
              : "—"
          }
          unit={sleepConsistency !== undefined ? "%" : ""}
          description={
            sleepConsistency !== undefined
              ? "clients meeting recovery target"
              : "No recovery data yet"
          }
          progress={sleepConsistency}
          progressLabel="Consistency"
          accent="#887DBC"
          icon={<Moon size={18} />}
        />

        <MetricCard
          title="Hydration"
          value={hydration !== undefined ? Math.round(clamp(hydration)) : "—"}
          unit={hydration !== undefined ? "%" : ""}
          description={
            hydration !== undefined
              ? "clients near daily target"
              : "No hydration data yet"
          }
          progress={hydration}
          progressLabel="Hydration"
          accent="#579FAC"
          icon={<Droplets size={18} />}
        />
      </section>

      {/* Signals */}
      <section className="bf-coach-signal-card">
        <div className="bf-coach-signal-head">
          <div>
            <div className="bf-kicker">CLIENT SIGNALS</div>
            <h4>Where to look next.</h4>
          </div>
          <Activity size={17} />
        </div>

        <div className="bf-coach-signal-grid">
          <Signal
            icon={CheckCircle2}
            title="Sessions on track"
            value={
              workoutCompletion !== undefined
                ? `${Math.round(clamp(workoutCompletion))}%`
                : "—"
            }
            accent="#B89257"
          />

          <Signal
            icon={HeartPulse}
            title="Recovery"
            value={
              sleepConsistency === undefined
                ? "—"
                : sleepConsistency < 70
                  ? "Needs review"
                  : "Stable"
            }
            accent={
              sleepConsistency !== undefined && sleepConsistency < 70
                ? "#C98268"
                : "#63B99B"
            }
          />

          <Signal
            icon={TrendingUp}
            title="Weekly adherence"
            value={
              adherence !== undefined
                ? `${Math.round(clamp(adherence))}%`
                : "—"
            }
            accent="#63B99B"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Weekly trend */}
      <section className="bf-weekly-card">
        <div className="bf-weekly-head">
          <div>
            <div className="bf-kicker">ROSTER RHYTHM</div>
            <h4>
              {hasWeeklyData
                ? "Consistency over intensity."
                : "Your roster rhythm will build here."}
            </h4>
          </div>
          <TrendingUp size={17} />
        </div>

        {hasWeeklyData ? (
          <div className="bf-week-bars">
            {weeklyBars.map((height, index) => (
              <div className="bf-week-column" key={index}>
                <div className="bf-week-track">
                  <div
                    className="bf-week-fill"
                    style={{
                      height: `${height}%`,
                      background:
                        index === 6
                          ? "linear-gradient(180deg,#B89257,#725A38)"
                          : "linear-gradient(180deg,#68AFC3,#315E67)",
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
                <span>{["M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S", "S"][index]}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="bf-week-empty">
            <span>No client activity recorded yet.</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </section>
  );
}

'use client';
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function DietAssigner({ clientId }: { clientId: string }) {
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<Record<string, string[]>>({});
  const [selectedItems, setSelectedItems] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [dietPlan, setDietPlan] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Form State
  const [meals, setMeals] = useState('3');
  const [goal, setGoal] = useState('fat_loss');
  const [calorieTier, setCalorieTier] = useState('1850');

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchMenu = async () => {
      try {
        const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DIET_API_URL;
        if (!url) throw new Error("API URL not configured in .env.local");

        const res = await fetch(url, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'get_menu', service: 'Diet' })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === 'success') setMenu(data.menu);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load menu database');
      }
    };
    fetchMenu();
  }, []);

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
      setError('Select at least one ingredient.');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DIET_API_URL!, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          service: 'Diet',
          action: 'generate_diet',
          preferences: {
            mealsCount: Number(meals),
            goal: goal,
            targetCalories: Number(calorieTier),
            mustInclude: selectedItems
          }
        })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      setDietPlan(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const assignToClient = () => {
    // Not connected to the backend yet. (No client IDs or diet plans are logged to the console.)
    alert('Diet assignment is not connected to the backend yet.');
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden mt-8">
      <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900">Diet Generation (Powered by AWS Optimizer)</h3>
      </div>
      
      <div className="p-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1">Client Goal</label>
            <select value={goal} onChange={(e) => setGoal(e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg outline-none">
              <option value="fat_loss">Fat Loss</option>
              <option value="maintenance">Maintenance</option>
              <option value="weight_gain">Weight Gain</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1">Target Calorie Tier</label>
            <select value={calorieTier} onChange={(e) => setCalorieTier(e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg outline-none">
              <option value="1550">1500 - 1600 kcal</option>
              <option value="1850">1800 - 1900 kcal</option>
              <option value="2150">2100 - 2200 kcal</option>
              <option value="2450">2400 - 2500 kcal</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1">Meal Split</label>
            <select value={meals} onChange={(e) => setMeals(e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg outline-none">
              <option value="2">2 Meals (Intermittent)</option>
              <option value="3">3 Meals</option>
              <option value="4">4 Meals</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-8">
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-3">Allowed Ingredients</label>
          <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-4 bg-slate-50 grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {Object.keys(menu).length === 0 ? <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">Loading S3 Database...</span> : null}
            {Object.entries(menu).map(([category, items]) => (
              items.map(item => (
                <label key={item} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm cursor-pointer text-slate-700">
                  <input 
                    type="checkbox" 
                    className="mt-1"
                    checked={selectedItems.includes(item)}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      if (e.target.checked) setSelectedItems([...selectedItems, item]);
                      else setSelectedItems(selectedItems.filter(i => i !== item));
                    }}
                  />
                  <span className="leading-tight">{item}</span>
                </label>
              ))
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <button 
          onClick={handleGenerate} 
          disabled={loading}
          className="w-full py-3 bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors flex justify-center items-center gap-2"
        >
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18}/> : 'Calculate Optimal Macros'}
        </button>

        {error && <p className="text-rose-600 text-sm mt-4">{error}</p>}

        {dietPlan && (
          <div className="mt-8 p-6 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={20}/> Plan Generated Successfully
                </h4>
                <p className="text-emerald-700 text-sm font-medium mt-1">
                  {dietPlan.summary['Total Calories']} kcal | {dietPlan.summary['Total Protein']}g Protein
                </p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={assignToClient} className="bg-emerald-600 text-white px-5 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shadow-sm hover:bg-emerald-700">
                Assign to Client
              </button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-emerald-100 overflow-hidden">
              <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                <thead className="bg-emerald-100/50 text-emerald-900 font-semibold">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="p-3">Ingredient</th>
                    <th className="p-3">Portion</th>
                    <th className="p-3">Macros</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-emerald-50">
                  {dietPlan.plan.map((item: any, idx: number) => (
                    <tr key={idx}>
                      <td className="p-3 font-medium text-slate-800">{item.Item}</td>
                      <td className="p-3 font-bold text-emerald-600">{item['Portion (g)']}g</td>
                      <td className="p-3 text-slate-500">{item.Calories} kcal, {item.Protein}g pro</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

type Exercise = { id: string; name: string; sets: number; reps: string; rest: string; notes: string };

export default function WorkoutBuilder() {
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([
    { id: '1', name: 'McGill Big 3 (Curl-up, Bird-Dog, Side Plank)', sets: 3, reps: '10s holds', rest: '60s', notes: 'Core warm-up. Do not skip.' },
    { id: '2', name: 'Supine Pelvic Tilts', sets: 3, reps: '12-15', rest: '45s', notes: 'Focus on lower abdominal engagement.' },
    { id: '3', name: 'Glute Bridges', sets: 4, reps: '15', rest: '60s', notes: 'Squeeze glutes at the top.' },
    { id: '4', name: 'Machine Chest Press', sets: 3, reps: '8-10', rest: '90s', notes: 'Controlled eccentric.' },
  ]);

  const addExercise = () => {
    setExercises([...exercises, { id: Date.now().toString(), name: '', sets: 3, reps: '10', rest: '60s', notes: '' }]);
  };

  const removeExercise = (id: string) => {
    setExercises(exercises.filter(ex => ex.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
      <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex justify-between items-center">

          <Plus size={16} /> Add Exercise
        </button>

        {exercises.map((ex, index) => (
          <div key={ex.id} className="flex gap-4 items-start group">

interface GlassCardProps {

  subtitle: string;
  statusText?: string;
  statusColor?: 'green' | 'red' | 'gold';
  Icon?: LucideIcon;
}

export function GlassCard({ title, subtitle, statusText, statusColor = 'green', Icon }: GlassCardProps) {
  const getStatusStyles = () => {
    switch (statusColor) {
      case 'green': return { color: '#00E27B', bg: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.1)', border: 'rgba(0, 226, 123, 0.3)' };

      case 'gold': return { color: '#E5B869', bg: 'rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.1)', border: 'rgba(229, 184, 105, 0.3)' };
    }
  };

  const statusStyle = getStatusStyles();

  return (
    <div className="plan-card fade-in">
      <div className="plan-card-left">
        <span className="plan-item-name">{title}</span>
        <span className="plan-item-category">{subtitle}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="plan-card-right" style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', gap: '6px' }}>
        {Icon && <Icon size={24} color={statusStyle.color} />}
        {statusText && (
          <span style={{ 
            fontSize: '10px', 
            fontWeight: '800', 
            color: statusStyle.color, 
            letterSpacing: '1px',
            background: statusStyle.bg,
            padding: '4px 8px',
            borderRadius: '4px',
            border: `1px solid ${statusStyle.border}`
          }}>
            {statusText.toUpperCase()}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

==> src/components/shared/MetricCard.tsx <==
"use client";

import React, { ReactNode, useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface MetricCardProps {
  title: string;
  value: number | string;
  unit?: string;
  description?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  progress?: number;
  progressLabel?: string;
  accent?: string;
  compact?: boolean;
}

export default function MetricCard({
  title,
  value,
  unit = "",
  description,
  icon,
  progress,
  progressLabel,
  accent = "#65BFA0",
  compact = false,
}: MetricCardProps) {
  const numericProgress =
    progress === undefined
      ? null
      : Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(progress) || 0));

  const [animatedProgress, setAnimatedProgress] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (numericProgress === null) return;

    let frame = 0;
    const start = performance.now();
    const duration = 850;

    setAnimatedProgress(0);

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const elapsed = time - start;
      const progressRatio = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progressRatio, 3);

      setAnimatedProgress(numericProgress * eased);

      if (progressRatio < 1) {
        frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }
    };

    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [numericProgress]);

  return (
    <div
      className={`metric-card bf-premium-surface ${compact ? "compact" : ""}`}
      style={{
        ["--metric-accent" as string]: accent,
        position: "relative",
        minHeight: compact ? "145px" : "170px",
        padding: compact ? "18px" : "21px",
        borderRadius: "22px",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.065)",
        background:
          "linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(4,5,6,0.985), rgba(0,0,0,0.99))",
        boxShadow:
          "inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.018), 0 18px 42px rgba(0,0,0,0.25), 0 0 22px rgba(104,199,161,0.018)",
        overflow: "hidden",
      } as React.CSSProperties}
    >
      <div
        className="bf-metric-ambient"
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          width: "120px",
          height: "120px",
          right: "-55px",
          top: "-55px",
          borderRadius: "50%",
          background: accent,
          opacity: 0.035,
          filter: "blur(30px)",
          pointerEvents: "none",
        }}
      />

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          marginBottom: compact ? "20px" : "24px",
          position: "relative",
          zIndex: 1,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: "10px",
            letterSpacing: "0.14em",
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </div>

        {icon && (
          <div
            style={{
              width: compact ? "34px" : "36px",
              height: compact ? "34px" : "36px",
              borderRadius: "12px",
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              justifyContent: "center",
              color: accent,
              background: `${accent}0e`,
              border: `1px solid ${accent}18`,
              boxShadow: `0 0 18px ${accent}08`,
            }}
          >
            {icon}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "baseline",
          gap: "6px",
          position: "relative",
          zIndex: 1,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: compact ? "29px" : "33px",
            fontWeight: 700,
            lineHeight: 1,
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
            letterSpacing: "-0.04em",
          }}
        >
          {value}
        </span>

        {unit && (
          <span
            style={{
              fontSize: "12px",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            }}
          >
            {unit}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {description && (
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: "10px",
            fontSize: "10px",
            color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            position: "relative",
            zIndex: 1,
          }}
        >
          {description}
        </div>
      )}

      {numericProgress !== null && (
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: compact ? "18px" : "21px",
            position: "relative",
            zIndex: 1,
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              height: "4px",
              borderRadius: "999px",
              background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
              overflow: "hidden",
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                height: "100%",
                width: `${animatedProgress}%`,
                borderRadius: "999px",
                background: accent,
                boxShadow: `0 0 7px ${accent}55`,
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              marginTop: "7px",
              fontSize: "9px",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            }}
          >
            <span>{progressLabel || "Progress"}</span>
            <span>{Math.round(animatedProgress)}%</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

type DataPoint = { date: string; [key: string]: string | number };

  dataKey: string;

  unit?: string;

            contentStyle={{ borderRadius: '8px', border: 'none', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.1)' }}
            formatter={(value: any) => [`${value} ${unit}`, dataKey.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + dataKey.slice(1)]}
          />
          <Area type="monotone" dataKey={dataKey} stroke={color} strokeWidth={3} fillOpacity={1} fill={`url(#gradient-${dataKey})`} />
        </AreaChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>

==> src/components/shared/ProgressRing.tsx <==
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface ProgressRingProps {
  value?: number;
  size?: number;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  label?: string;
  subLabel?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  duration?: number;
  accent?: string;
  glow?: number;
}

export default function ProgressRing({
  value,
  size = 150,
  strokeWidth = 10,
  label,
  subLabel,
  suffix = "",
  duration = 1000,
  accent = "#65BFA0",
  glow = 2,
}: ProgressRingProps) {
  const hasValue = value !== undefined && value !== null && Number.isFinite(Number(value));
  const safeValue = hasValue
    ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(value)))
    : 0;

  const [animatedValue, setAnimatedValue] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0;

    if (!hasValue) {
      setAnimatedValue(0);
      return () => undefined;
    }

    const start = performance.now();

    setAnimatedValue(0);

    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const elapsed = time - start;
      const ratio = duration <= 0 ? 1 : Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - ratio, 3);

      setAnimatedValue(safeValue * eased);

      if (ratio < 1) {
        frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }
    };

    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [safeValue, duration, hasValue]);

  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const dashOffset = circumference - (animatedValue / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "relative",
        width: size,
        height: size,
        flexShrink: 0,
        color: accent,
      }}
      aria-label={`${label ?? "Progress"}: ${hasValue ? `${Math.round(safeValue)} percent` : "no data"}`}
    >
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        style={{
          transform: "rotate(-90deg)",
          overflow: "visible",
        }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.055)"
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        />

        {hasValue && (
          <circle
            cx={size / 2}
            cy={size / 2}
            r={radius}
            fill="none"
            stroke={accent}
            strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeDasharray={circumference}
            strokeDashoffset={dashOffset}
            style={{
              transition: "stroke-dashoffset 80ms linear",
              filter:
                glow > 0
                  ? `drop-shadow(0 0 ${glow}px ${accent}55)`
                  : "none",
            }}
          />
        )}
      </svg>

      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          textAlign: "center",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: size > 130 ? "28px" : "21px",
            fontWeight: 700,
            lineHeight: 1,
            color: "var(--text-primary)",
            letterSpacing: "-0.04em",
          }}
        >
          {hasValue ? Math.round(animatedValue) : "—"}
          {hasValue && suffix}
        </div>

        {label && (
          <div
            style={{
              marginTop: "7px",
              fontSize: "9px",
              letterSpacing: "0.15em",
              textTransform: "uppercase",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            }}
          >
            {label}
          </div>
        )}

        {subLabel && (
          <div
            style={{
              marginTop: "3px",
              fontSize: "9px",
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
            }}
          >
            {hasValue ? subLabel : "No data yet"}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "cn"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "group/alert relative grid w-full gap-0.5 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-sm has-data-[slot=alert-action]:relative has-data-[slot=alert-action]:pr-18 has-[>svg]:grid-cols-[auto_1fr] has-[>svg]:gap-x-2 *:[svg]:row-span-2 *:[svg]:translate-y-0.5 *:[svg]:text-current *:[svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-card text-card-foreground",
        destructive:
          "bg-card text-destructive *:data-[slot=alert-description]:text-destructive/90 *:[svg]:text-current",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Alert({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-medium group-has-[>svg]/alert:col-start-2 [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-balance text-muted-foreground md:text-pretty [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground [&_p:not(:last-child)]:mb-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertAction({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-action"
      className={cn("absolute top-2 right-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription, AlertAction }

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { Slot } from "radix-ui"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "group/button inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 active:not-aria-[haspopup]:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-3 aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/80",
        outline:
          "border-border bg-background hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklch,var(--secondary),var(--foreground)_5%)] aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default:
          "h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        xs: "h-6 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)] px-2 text-xs in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        sm: "h-7 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),12px)] px-2.5 text-[0.8rem] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",
        lg: "h-9 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        icon: "size-8",
        "icon-xs":
          "size-6 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        "icon-sm":
          "size-7 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),12px)] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg",
        "icon-lg": "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"button"> &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    asChild?: boolean
  }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button"

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }

==> src/components/ui/dialog.tsx <==
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"

function Dialog({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="dialog" {...props} />
}

function DialogTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dialog-trigger" {...props} />
}

function DialogPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dialog-portal" {...props} />
}

function DialogClose({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Close>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" {...props} />
}

function DialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 isolate z-50 bg-black/10 duration-100 supports-backdrop-filter:backdrop-blur-xs data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogContent({
  className,
  children,
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPortal>
      <DialogOverlay />
      <DialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="dialog-content"
        className={cn(
          "fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 z-50 grid w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 gap-4 rounded-xl bg-popover p-4 text-sm text-popover-foreground ring-1 ring-foreground/10 duration-100 outline-none sm:max-w-sm data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-open:zoom-in-95 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0 data-closed:zoom-out-95",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" asChild>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              className="absolute top-2 right-2"
              size="icon-sm"
            >
              <XIcon
              />
              <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
            </Button>
          </DialogPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    </DialogPortal>
  )
}

function DialogHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogFooter({
  className,
  showCloseButton = false,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-footer"
      className={cn(
        "-mx-4 -mb-4 flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 rounded-b-xl border-t bg-muted/50 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      {showCloseButton && (
        <DialogPrimitive.Close asChild>
          <Button variant="outline">Close</Button>
        </DialogPrimitive.Close>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function DialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="dialog-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-heading text-base leading-none font-medium",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="dialog-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-muted-foreground *:[a]:underline *:[a]:underline-offset-3 *:[a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogOverlay,
  DialogPortal,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
}

"use client"

import { useTheme } from "next-themes"
import { Toaster as Sonner, type ToasterProps } from "sonner"
import { CircleCheckIcon, InfoIcon, TriangleAlertIcon, OctagonXIcon, Loader2Icon } from "lucide-react"

const Toaster = ({ ...props }: ToasterProps) => {
  const { theme = "system" } = useTheme()

  return (
    <Sonner
      theme={theme as ToasterProps["theme"]}
      className="toaster group"
      icons={{
        success: (
          <CircleCheckIcon className="size-4" />
        ),
        info: (
          <InfoIcon className="size-4" />
        ),
        warning: (
          <TriangleAlertIcon className="size-4" />
        ),
        error: (
          <OctagonXIcon className="size-4" />
        ),
        loading: (
          <Loader2Icon className="size-4 animate-spin" />
        ),
      }}
      style={
        {
          "--normal-bg": "var(--popover)",
          "--normal-text": "var(--popover-foreground)",
          "--normal-border": "var(--border)",
          "--border-radius": "var(--radius)",
        } as React.CSSProperties
      }
      toastOptions={{
        classNames: {
          toast: "cn-toast",
        },
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Toaster }

  )

      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"caption">) {
  return (

      data-slot="table-caption"

  )
}

}

==> src/database/schema.ts <==
// Define the core keys every item in the table will share
export interface DynamoDBBaseItem {
  PK: string;
  SK: string;
  GSI1PK?: string;
  GSI1SK?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

// Client Profile Entity
export interface ClientProfile extends DynamoDBBaseItem {
  type: 'CLIENT';
  coachId: string; // Used to build GSI1PK
  name: string;
  email: string;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
  baseline: {
    weight: number;
    height: number;
    goals: string;
  };
}

// Daily Tracking Log Entity
export interface DailyLog extends DynamoDBBaseItem {
  type: 'DAILY_LOG';
  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  adherenceScore: number;
  tasks: {
    water: number;
    steps: number;
    proteinHit: boolean;
    workoutCompleted: boolean;
  };
}

// Diet Plan Assignment Entity
export interface DietPlan extends DynamoDBBaseItem {
  type: 'DIET_PLAN';
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'ARCHIVED';
  targetCalories: number;
  targetProtein: number;
  mealsCount: number;
  items: Array<{
    name: string;
    portionGrams: number;
    calories: number;
    protein: number;
  }>;
}

==> src/services/api.ts <==
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ACTIVE API LAYER
//  - The API address comes ONLY from NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL (no address in source code).
//  - After sign-in, every request carries the session token as "Authorization: Bearer ...".
//  - The server enforces who may see what; the checks in the UI are for convenience only.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const API_BASE_URL = (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || '').replace(/\/$/, '');

export interface UserSession {
  PK: string;
  userCode: string;
  role: 'CLIENT' | 'COACH' | 'ADMIN';
  name: string;
  email?: string;
  mustChangePassword?: boolean;
  onboardingCompleted?: boolean;
  token?: string;
}

export type CoachClient = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  goal: string;
  adherence: number;
  lastCheckIn: string;
  status: 'on-track' | 'needs-attention';
};

const SESSION_KEY = 'bluefit_user_session';

export const authSession = {
  get: (): UserSession | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },
  set: (session: UserSession) => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('bluefit:auth-change'));
  },
  clear: () => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('bluefit:auth-change'));
  },
};

function getLocalDateISO(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  return `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

// The signed-in user's own ID. Pages never choose whose data to load.
function sessionId(): string {
  const id = authSession.get()?.PK;
  if (!id) throw new Error('Please sign in to continue.');
  return id;
}

async function apiFetch<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  if (!API_BASE_URL) {
    throw new Error('The API address is not configured (set NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL).');
  }
  const token = authSession.get()?.token;

  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...(options.headers || {}),
    },
  });

  if (res.status === 401 && token && !path.startsWith('/auth/')) {
    authSession.clear(); // expired / revoked session: the layout sends the user back to sign-in
    throw new Error('Your session has expired. Please sign in again.');
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    let errorDetail = `Request failed (${res.status})`;
    try {
      const errJson = await res.json();
      if (typeof errJson.detail === 'string') errorDetail = errJson.detail;
      else if (Array.isArray(errJson.detail)) errorDetail = 'Please check the information you entered.';
      else if (typeof errJson.message === 'string') errorDetail = errJson.message;
    } catch {}
    throw new Error(errorDetail);
  }
  return res.json();
}

export const dashboardAPI = {
  // --- AUTH & FIRST-TIME SETUP ---
  login: async (role: 'CLIENT' | 'COACH' | 'ADMIN', identifier: string, password: string) => {
    const user = await apiFetch<UserSession>('/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ role, identifier, password }),
    });
    // A temporary-password login has no token yet: keep it in memory only, never in storage
    if (user.token && !user.mustChangePassword) authSession.set(user);
    return user;
  },

  completeFirstTimeSetup: async (payload: {
    userId: string;
    currentPassword: string;
    newPassword: string;
    [key: string]: any;
  }) => {
    const res = await apiFetch<{ message: string; user: UserSession }>('/auth/complete-setup', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
    authSession.set(res.user);
    return res.user;
  },

  // --- ADMIN PROVISIONING & MANAGEMENT (admin session required) ---
  saveOnboarding: (payload: {
    name: string;
    role: 'CLIENT' | 'COACH' | string;
    email: string;
    [key: string]: any;
  }) =>
    apiFetch<{
      message: string;
      userId: string;
      userCode: string;
      tempPassword: string;
      role: 'CLIENT' | 'COACH';
      name: string;
    }>('/admin/onboarding', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),

  getAllUsers: () => apiFetch<any[]>('/admin/users'),
  getAdminData: () => apiFetch<{ stats: any[] }>('/admin/dashboard/stats'),
  deleteUser: (userId: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string }>(`/admin/users/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
    }),
  assignCoach: (clientId: string, coachId: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string; updated: any }>(
      `/admin/users/${encodeURIComponent(clientId)}/assign`,
      {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({ coachId }),
      }
    ),

  // --- COACH (always the signed-in coach's own data) ---
  getCoachData: (_coachId?: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ stats: any[]; recentActivity: any[] }>(
      `/coach/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId())}/dashboard`
    ),
  getCoachClients: (_coachId?: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ clients: CoachClient[] }>(
      `/coach/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId())}/clients`
    ),
  getCoachTemplates: (_coachId?: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ templates: any[] }>(
      `/coach/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId())}/templates`
    ),
  createTemplate: (payload: any, _coachId?: string) =>
    apiFetch<any>(`/coach/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId())}/templates`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),
  getTemplate: (templateId: string) =>
    apiFetch<any>(`/coach/templates/${encodeURIComponent(templateId)}`),
  updateTemplate: (templateId: string, payload: any) =>
    apiFetch<any>(`/coach/templates/${encodeURIComponent(templateId)}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),

  // --- ATHLETE (CLIENT) ---
  getAthleteData: (_athleteId?: string, date?: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ date: string; todayTraining: any[] }>(
      `/athlete/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId())}/dashboard?date=${encodeURIComponent(date || getLocalDateISO())}`
    ),
  saveCheckin: (payload: {
    weight: number;
    adherence: string;
    workoutQuality: string;
    waist?: number;
    notes?: string;
  }) =>
    apiFetch<{ message: string; checkinId: string }>('/athlete/checkin', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload), // the server uses the signed-in athlete, not an ID from the browser
    }),
};
